The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [293] [Filter Learning for Subgraphs: Algebras and Performance Risk Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21263)
*Purui Zhang,Feng Ji,Yanan Zhao,Bihan Wen,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种子图滤波器学习（SFL）框架，利用距离感知拉普拉斯构造定义子图滤波器代数，在部分观测下逼近全局图谱滤波器，实验证明优于多项式滤波、分布无关算子和直接数值学习等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中完整图拓扑往往不可得，现有谱图信号处理任务通常假设已知完整图结构，限制了在部分观测场景下的应用。

Method: 将SFL形式化为统计学习问题，基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造建立子图滤波器代数，定义结构可控的滤波器类，并在最小二乘损失下建立性能风险界。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提代数模型在SFL任务中一致优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子以及直接数值滤波器学习基线。

Conclusion: 所提子图滤波器学习框架能有效利用部分观测信息逼近全局滤波器，具有理论保障和实际优越性能。

Abstract: Graph signal processing tasks that leverage spectral information typically assume access to the complete graph topology, which is often unavailable in practice. We propose a systematic framework for subgraph filter learning (SFL), where subgraph-supported operators approximate ambient graph filters under partial observations. We formulate SFL as a statistical learning problem in which optimal subgraph operators are inherently data-dependent. To address the difficulty of directly estimating such operators, we develop a subgraph filter algebra based on distance-aware Laplacian constructions, defining a structured and controllable class of filters for effective approximation. We further establish performance risk bounds under the least squares loss, quantifying how well the learned operator approximates the restricted ambient mapping. Experiments real-world datasets show that, for SFL tasks, the proposed algebraic models consistently outperform polynomial filters, distribution-agnostic operators, and direct numerical filter learning baselines that attempt to recover the underlying structure from data.

</details>


### [294] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个反直觉现象：在基于组归一化强化学习（GRPO）中，对LLM智能体施加密集的每一步预测奖励（预测下一观察）不仅无益，反而会彻底摧毁策略——智能体会陷入"暗室病态"（预测准确率趋近1，任务成功率为0）。原因在于GRPO的z分数归一化会将有界奖励转化为无界压力，而密集信号的方差随掌握程度衰减的特性决定了其是否安全。进一步实验表明，将相同信号通过辅助损失通道而非奖励通道传递，可提升约20个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励、长视野LLM智能体面临信用分配困难，密集的每一步监督（如预测下一观察）被视为有希望的解决方案。作者希望验证这一直觉是否有效。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B模型上，以ALFWorld为环境，使用基于势能的预测奖励结合GRPO进行训练。通过单因素消融（去除std归一化）、信号通道对比（奖励通道vs辅助损失通道）及方差轮廓分析来定位问题根源。

Result: 1) 预测奖励在GRPO下导致零任务成功率，智能体陷入"暗室病态"；2) 仅移除GRPO的std归一化即可将性能从0%恢复至基线水平；3) 相同信号通过辅助损失通道可提升约20点，而奖励通道效果最差为中性；4) 打乱的金标签（shuffled-gold）与真实金标签表现一致，说明增益不依赖正确标签。

Conclusion: 密集预测奖励在GRPO下不是无效而是有害；z分数归一化会放大小组内方差，当全失败组占主导时，有界奖励变为无界压力；方差轮廓准则可预测哪些密集信号安全；信号通道的选择比信号本身更重要——辅助损失通道优于奖励通道。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [295] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务学习在基于游戏状态数据的预测任务中的应用，提出了一个结合视觉、上下文和单位状态信息的多模态架构，通过在《坦克世界》数据集上实验验证了多任务学习相比单任务模型在泛化性和效率上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏遥测数据提供多个相关监督信号，传统单任务模型独立训练成本高且忽略任务间共享信息，因此研究多任务学习能否在提升泛化能力的同时降低训练和推理成本。

Method: 采用多模态架构，结合栅格化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和单位状态信息，通过图像编码器和注意力机制进行交互建模；对比单任务与多任务训练，评估损失加权策略和梯度冲突处理，并在有限数据下测试预训练/微调效果。

Result: 在《坦克世界》大规模数据集上，多任务模型相比单任务模型在泛化性和效率上表现更优，同时实验验证了损失加权策略和预训练/微调的有效性。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在基于游戏状态数据的预测任务中具有显著优势，既能提升泛化能力又能降低计算成本，未来可进一步探索更复杂的任务交互和迁移场景。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [296] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M³-Gen框架，通过条件生成对抗网络，利用组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，解决了多模态研究中基因数据获取受限的问题，并在TCGA数据集上验证了其生成数据的真实性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据获取成本高、隐私限制大，限制了多模态研究和AI应用的发展，因此需要一种能够从易获取的临床数据和病理图像中生成基因表达谱的方法。

Method: 采用条件生成对抗网络（GAN），通过对比学习从临床变量和病理图像中学习统一潜在表征，利用双模态嵌入引导生成模型产生生物学上连贯的基因表达谱，并引入注意力机制实现内在可解释性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上，M³-Gen能够生成真实且功能上有意义的基因表达数据，并通过注意力机制识别出病理图像中对特定基因表达生成影响最大的区域。

Conclusion: M³-Gen有效解决了基因表达数据获取的瓶颈问题，提供了一种可解释的多模态生成框架，有望推动精准医学中多模态数据整合研究。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [297] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信息论方法直接测量LoRA适配器在冻结基座模型上写入的比特数，发现其记忆容量远低于全参数微调，且与参数位置高度相关：MLP层容量约为注意力层的两倍。实验表明，监督微调会泄露隐私，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习则不会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"LoRA适配器仅代表技能而非数据记录"这一普遍假设，量化微调过程中模型究竟记住了多少训练数据信息。

Method: 将基于压缩的记忆化分析方法扩展到冻结基座模型场景，直接测量低秩适配器写入模型的比特数，分析参数位置（注意力层vs MLP层）和训练方式（监督微调vs强化学习）的影响。

Result: 每个可训练参数仅存储数比特信息，远低于完整模型容量；MLP层容量约为注意力层的两倍；监督微调会复制训练数据秘密，而强化学习则不会记录。

Conclusion: 微调记忆量是可测量的设计指标，而非事后攻击目标。该量化方法将"微调写入什么"从经验性认知转变为可对抗设计的定量依据。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [298] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制消融实验发现，SalUn等基于显著性掩码的机器遗忘方法在表示层面上并非真正有效，其表现与随机掩码或无约束更新在统计上无显著差异。遗忘主要由梯度在最后一层的集中分布（~92%）和表示几何结构主导，而非权重选择策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度显著性选择权重的遗忘方法被广泛使用，但其对表示层面遗忘的实际贡献尚不明确。本文旨在通过严格的控制实验，厘清显著性掩码机制是否真正起到作用。

Method: 在CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18进行匹配计算量的实验设计，对比显著性掩码、随机掩码（同等稀疏度）和无约束更新三种策略，保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算固定。使用线性探测、原型恢复和层间CKA等多种表示层面评估方法。

Result: 三种配置在表示层恢复性上统计等效。遗忘梯度高度集中在最后一层（CIFAR-10上约92%的平方梯度能量）。显著性掩码的类别特异性很低（特异性指数0.09-0.11），不同遗忘类别选择高度重叠的参数子集。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，表示层遗忘主要由梯度集中度和表示几何结构主导，而非显著性选择的特定权重。有效的表示层遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的优化目标，而非更复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [299] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在窄域有害数据上微调对齐语言模型会导致广泛失对齐，原因是微调激活了预存在模型中的“人格”子空间结构。该子空间在无关领域共享一个低秩核心，移除它能彻底阻止失对齐，注入它则诱发失对齐，且该结构无法通过权重编辑消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究窄域微调为何会泛化到无关领域（即“涌现性失对齐”现象）的根本原因，而非仅仅描述该现象。

Method: 1. 从冻结的Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct中通过对比教师强制提取每个域的人格子空间。2. 分析子空间的核心结构（低秩核心、风格核心）。3. 对比不同框架（不安全代码vs教育性代码）的微调效果。4. 通过投射/注入子空间进行因果干预。5. 尝试权重编辑和梯度投影等消融实验。

Result: 1. 4个无关领域共享一个低秩核心，显著偏离随机子空间（657倍）。2. 不安全代码的第一步优化就比教育性代码更强烈地走向失对齐。3. 投射出子空间完全阻止失对齐（27.7%→0.0%），注入则诱发失对齐（达45.4%）。4. 权重编辑无法移除该结构，被清除的结构会在子空间内重建。

Conclusion: 窄域微调导致广泛失对齐的原因是激活了模型中预存的人格子空间结构，该结构是因果充分的。当前的权重编辑手段无法消除它，但通过子空间投射可以阻止其影响，不过同时也消除了窄域学习效果。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [300] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法，通过预测干净token的后验均值并利用精确的核依赖线性映射转换为得分，解决了离散扩散模型中得分比率违反贝叶斯可实现性的问题。该方法消除了负权重，在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD)要求得分比率为正，但正性并不保证贝叶斯可实现性——噪声状态下的比率未必由任何干净token后验经前向核共同诱导。训练后的模型中大量得分向量违反约束或与有效后验不兼容，导致有限步采样中出现负权重。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法：首先预测干净token的后验均值（定义在概率单形上），然后通过一个精确的、依赖前向核的线性映射将其转换为得分。该方法适用于任何满足温和支撑条件的坐标连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀破坏，概率单形映射到桥接多面体；对于吸收掩码破坏，该目标精确恢复MD4。

Result: 原始得分投影到桥接多面体后消除所有负权重，生成PPL从203.6降至175.1。CIFAR-10上28.4M参数模型：测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著改善。170M参数OpenWebText模型在128步时生成PPL达143.3，优于纯均匀SEDD的183.6等基线。

Conclusion: M2S通过构造性方法确保得分的贝叶斯可实现性，在理论上具有普适性，在实践中显著提升了离散扩散模型的生成质量，有望成为离散扩散模型的新标准范式。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [301] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐无法识别已丢失信息，会导致注意力输出误差不可控；随机化驱逐（泊松采样尾部+ Hájek校正）可恢复可识别性，并提供每步误差证书（经验覆盖率0.97）。实验验证了七个预注册声明，其中三个被否定，最终发现随机化带来的不是预测能力，而是归因能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性KV缓存驱逐（保留top-k重要token）存在根本性缺陷：无法知道被驱逐的值是否被篡改，导致注意力输出误差无法通过服务时估计器一致地估计。

Method: 提出随机化驱逐方法：对尾部token进行泊松采样（已知包含概率），在softmax中引入Hájek校正（一个logit偏移），并基于调查抽样方差估计构造每步误差证书。

Result: 误差证书经验覆盖率达0.97且不损失精度；7个预注册声明中3个被否定（问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎无成本、输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败、证书门控预算扩展无效）；证书能区分缓存导致和固有的失败（AUC 0.73-0.75 vs 输出置信度0.47-0.54）。

Conclusion: 随机化驱逐不能预测失败，但提供了归因能力——证书可用于判断失败是由缓存驱逐还是模型本身引起，从而指导重新计算调度，优于随机或置信度门控策略。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [302] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种条件时间序列扩散模型构建“JITAI-数字孪生”的方法，通过在三个步骤（预训练、微调、推理时校准）中整合观测数据、既往干预数据和专家知识，生成目标子群体的数字孪生体，用于在实际部署前测试和比较在线算法，并在HeartSteps系列研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中在线算法的设计不佳可能给参与者带来负担，因此在部署前需要借助逼真的模拟用户来评估候选算法，但目前缺乏能够生成目标子群体数字孪生的有效方法。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型，确保时间一致性（未来动作不影响生成的过去）；通过三步更新：在大型观测数据集上预训练、在相关人群的小规模既往干预数据上微调、利用领域专家知识在推理时校准到目标人群。

Result: 在HeartSteps v2到v4系列的物理活动建议干预部署中，该方法在再现目标子群体的时间结构和参与者间结构方面优于更简单的模拟器。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在目标部署运行前进行有效模拟，为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供了前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [303] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MTP草案头在超长上下文（百万token）下因全注意力机制产生巨大开销的问题，提出Windowed-MTP：仅在草案注意力层应用滑动窗口+注意力汇聚，保持目标模型全注意力验证不变。该方案无需训练、即插即用、无损失，将草案KV工作集限定为常数，减少约99%的KV条目，在单GPU上实现每解码步成本降低28%-44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型内置的多Token预测（MTP/NEXTN）草案头在长上下文场景下，其全注意力机制需要对整个KV缓存进行计算，导致草案开销随上下文线性增长，在百万token级别时草案成本主导推理，甚至使投机解码比不投机更慢。

Method: 借鉴StreamingLLM的滑动窗口+注意力汇聚思想，仅对草案注意力层进行窗口化处理（Windowed-MTP），保留目标模型的全注意力验证不变。由于最终由目标模型决定接受哪些token，窗口化只影响提案内容，不影响已接受token的正确性，因此本质上是无损失的。

Result: 在Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B和Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B三种架构、百万token上下文、单GPU SGLang环境下，每解码步成本比原生MTP草案降低28%-44%。未读取的草案KV（占总KV的7.7%-11%）可通过紧凑环形缓冲区回收，且不影响接受率或生成质量。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP有效解决了长上下文场景下MTP草案头的注意力开销问题，是一种训练免费、即插即用、无损的优化方案，显著提升投机解码在超长上下文中的效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [304] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部上下文信息的离散流匹配方法，通过上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失函数，在无需显著增加计算开销的情况下，将生成困惑度降低最多63%，并支持任意顺序生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标暴露模型于难度各异的token（从低熵可预测到高熵模糊），标准方法无法区分这些token，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出两种简单改进：(1) 上下文加权采样器，修改连续时间马尔可夫链以融入局部上下文信息；(2) 缩放交叉熵损失函数，根据不同token的上下文密度重新加权训练信号。

Result: 在OpenWebText上生成困惑度降低最多63%，质量匹敌强半自回归块扩散基线，且计算开销可忽略，同时保留任意顺序生成能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的重要因素，简单的上下文感知改进即可显著提升采样和训练效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [305] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构的可逆变换用于扩散Transformer的W4A4后训练量化，在保持低推理成本的同时有效处理激活值中的异常值，比现有方法更快且量化质量更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer后训练量化到W4A4时，激活值中的异常值导致4bit表示质量严重下降。已有的可逆线性变换方案要么计算成本低但量化精度差（如SmoothQuant），要么量化精度好但在线计算成本高（如Hadamard变换或全稠密矩阵）。需要一种兼顾量化精度和推理效率的方案。

Method: 提出KroQuant方法：对激活值的每个32元素块应用学习的Kronecker结构可逆变换，参数少于per-channel scaling的一半；块局部结构可通过小型tensor-core GEMM高效运行；配合离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器核比SmoothQuant核快14%；在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell等模型上（W4A4，MXFP4e2），在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上输出比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换成功平衡了扩散Transformer量化中的精度与效率，实现了更快的推理速度和更好的量化质量。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [306] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTEL算法，通过token级错误定位实现更高效的推理时扩展，在多项任务上以更少计算量取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（如独立采样、多轮对话）缺乏token级别的信用分配，有效推理前缀常被丢弃，导致计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL利用固定反馈或环境反馈进行token级错误定位，通过比较条件概率（有信息反馈 vs 无信息基线）识别错误发生步骤，截断轨迹并重新生成，最大化复用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，TTEL以约一半token量（360.4k vs 735.0k）达到pass@64=71.0%；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上全面优于其他基线方法。

Conclusion: TTEL在顺序推理领域建立了严格占优的帕累托前沿，验证了token级错误定位在推理时扩展中的有效性。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [307] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层深度强化学习架构MAPS，通过中央Master agent生成全局协调策略的紧凑嵌入（proto-plan），并由分散的Worker agent执行车辆级控制，在无信号交叉口实现多车协调，在72种交叉口配置中实现了无碰撞导航并显著降低了平均旅行时间，且在零样本迁移到更多车辆时保持94%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在无信号交叉口协调中面临组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或刚性智能体设计等挑战，需要更高效的协调机制。

Method: 提出Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS)，一种分层深度强化学习架构：中央Master agent生成连续紧凑的proto-plan（编码全局协调策略），分散的Worker agent将proto-plan与局部观测结合执行车辆级控制，实现策略意图与战术执行的解耦。

Result: 在HighwayEnv的72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航，平均旅行时间显著优于SOTA基线；3 agent训练的系统零样本迁移到5 agent场景时达到94%成功率。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车协调提供了有前景的框架，能够实现有效的策略泛化和跨场景迁移。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [308] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态策略蒸馏框架，通过文本教师模型指导音频-语言学生模型进行深度逻辑推理，并构建三层对称语料库，显著提升音频推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上取得进展，但逻辑推理能力远落后于纯文本大模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量的音频推理训练数据。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD框架：1) 学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型以匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；2) 构建三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），将跨模态蒸馏扩展至非语言线索推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR四个基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频推理能力和思维链质量，同时在领域迁移下较好地保持了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态蒸馏策略能有效将文本大模型的逻辑推理能力迁移至音频-语言模型，且通过精心设计的多层次语料可覆盖超越纯文本可恢复内容的复杂声学推理场景。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [309] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EnsembleEGNN，通过编码分子构象集合提升环肽性质预测性能，预训练模型优于序列基线，混合模型进一步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测通常使用单一构象，但分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在，忽略这一信息限制了预测精度。

Method: 先使用共享EGNN层编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block池化得到整体表示；在CREMP数据集上多任务自监督预训练（掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建、成对距离重建）。

Result: 预训练EnsembleEGNN在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB上R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699，优于BERT基线（R²=0.439, r=0.667）；与BERT联合训练后R²=0.538, r=0.737。

Conclusion: 编码构象集合为热力学有效嵌入可显著提升环肽性质预测性能，且与序列编码器互补。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [310] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimePNS，一个基于反事实必要性推理的时间序列可解释性框架，通过两阶段设计（因果生成学习+反事实干预）识别对模型预测既充分又必要的关键子序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向充分性的解释方法可能将支持预测但非必要的虚假子序列赋予高重要性，缺乏对必要性的考量。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程与充分性解释掩码；Stage II对时间因子进行反事实干预以获得必要性信号，监督时间门控机制优化初始解释。

Result: 在合成与真实时间序列基准上，TimePNS更准确识别决策关键子序列，在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: 必要性感知（反事实干预）是提升时间序列解释质量的关键，能够有效抑制非必要成分、突出反事实必要的子序列。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [311] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows)和Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，一种通过维度递增的插值路径实现变尺寸生成（如图、序列）的流模型框架，在连续和离散空间中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流模型（如Flow Matching等）受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出尺寸本身就是可学习自由度的场景（如变尺寸图生成、变长序列生成）。

Method: (1) 定义Expanding Interpolant：沿时间维度逐步增广状态空间（添加条件噪声）；(2) 提出EFMs：将任意两时间步间的映射分解为expand operator（增广状态空间）和transport map（沿插值路径前推），两者组合实现联合扩维与去噪；(3) 扩展到离散单纯形，支持变尺寸图和变长序列生成。

Result: EFMs在连续和离散模态上均有效，能够处理变尺寸生成任务；当expand operator为恒等映射时，退化为传统固定画布的流模型。

Conclusion: EFLows和EFMs为输出尺寸作为可学习自由度的场景提供了一个原则性框架，统一了固定尺寸和可变尺寸生成。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [SPECTRA: State-Space Exogenous Context and Temporal-Frequency Resolution Architecture for Probabilistic Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20587)
*Hang Ye,Xinyan Jiang,Yuedong Shi,Yangxin Zhu,Jianming Wei,Tian Zheng,Xiaoying Zheng,Yongxin Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构，通过自适应分离确定性趋势与随机波动，分别建模预测基线与不确定性分布，在负荷、电价、太阳能和风电预测的18个设置中取得14个最优CRPS，平均降低5.74%，上尾分位风险降低7.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率能源预测方法将多尺度分解、外生变量对齐和概率输出作为分离步骤，未能揭示可预测结构与不确定性波动如何共同塑造预测分布。本文旨在提出一种将确定性-随机分离作为核心设计原则的统一架构。

Method: 提出状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构：(1) 自适应分离确定性流与残差流；(2) 将外生上下文与两者对齐；(3) 通过多分辨率谱-时域状态空间建模优化确定性主干；(4) 从互补表示中估计有序分位边界。

Result: 在负荷、电价、太阳能和风电预测的18个设置中，14个取得最优连续排名概率评分（CRPS），平均CRPS降低5.74%，上尾分位风险降低7.27%。

Conclusion: 确定性-随机分离是通用概率能源预测的有效设计原则，验证了趋势-周期分量决定基线轨迹、高频残差和外生扰动主导预测不确定性分布的核心假设。

Abstract: Modern power systems increasingly require probabilistic forecasts amid interacting uncertainties from renewable intermittency, flexible demand, market volatility, and weather-dependent generation. However, existing methods often treat multi-scale decomposition, exogenous-variable alignment, and probabilistic output as separate steps, obscuring how predictable structures and uncertainty-bearing fluctuations jointly shape the forecast distribution. This paper proposes a state-space exogenous-context and temporal-frequency resolution architecture for general probabilistic energy forecasting. Its central premise is that trend-periodic components primarily determine the baseline trajectory, whereas high-frequency residuals and external perturbations govern the spread and asymmetry of forecast uncertainty. Accordingly, the architecture adaptively separates deterministic and residual streams, aligns exogenous context with both, refines the deterministic backbone through multi-resolution spectral-temporal state-space modeling, and estimates ordered quantile boundaries from their complementary representations. Experiments on load, price, solar, and wind forecasting achieve the best continuous ranked probability score in 14 of 18 settings, reducing average CRPS by 5.74\% and upper-tail quantile risk by 7.27\% over the strongest baselines. These results support deterministic-stochastic separation as an effective design principle for general probabilistic energy forecasting.

</details>


### [313] [Automatic knot selection in smooth additive models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21083)
*Nicolás Carrizosa,Vanesa Guerrero,María Durbán*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的GAM显式结选择方法（扩展A-splines），结合Fellner-Schall调参，相比P-splines能用更少的基函数达到相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-spline回归中结的数量和位置选择至关重要，传统方法要么用正则化（如P-splines）自动调节光滑度，要么用结选择算法。后者虽在某些场景有优势，但因计算或建模限制常被忽视，作者希望开发一种实用的显式结选择方法。

Method: 基于自适应样条（A-splines）结选择方法进行扩展，结合定制的Fellner-Schall方案来调优相关参数，应用于广义加性模型（GAMs）。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法与P-splines及最先进的结选择技术性能相当，但模型使用的基函数数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 显式结选择方法在保持预测性能的同时，能构建更稀疏、更简洁的模型，在特定场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: B-spline regression constitutes a widely used framework for nonparametric modeling. The performance of this methodology depends on specifying the number and placement of changepoints, known as knots, prior to the estimation process. Such knot sequence determines the dimension of the B-spline basis used to represent the regression function and the number of coefficients to be estimated. Therefore, the knots' choice affects the model's flexibility, influencing its smoothness and goodness-of-fit. Traditionally, this problem has been addressed either by explicitly selecting knots, via knot-selection algorithms, or by regularization methods, such as P-splines, which automatically tune the regressor's smoothness. The latter have become the standard in generalized additive models (GAMs). In contrast, knot-selection techniques, frequently neglected because of computational or modeling limitations, provide certain advantages which can be valuable in some contexts. In this work, we introduce a novel explicit knot-selection technique for GAMs based on an extension of the adaptive splines (A-splines) knot selection methodology, combined with a customized Fellner-Schall scheme for tuning the associated parameters. Our approach is evaluated on various synthetic and real datasets and compared with P-splines and state-of-the-art knot-selection techniques. The results indicate comparable performance, while producing models built on a substantially smaller number of basis elements.

</details>


### [314] [Transformer-based Diffusion models for Hydrological Time Series Probabilistic Imputation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21200)
*Ferdinand Bhavsar,Lionel Benoit,Maxime Savatier,Edith Gabriel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的扩散模型，用于水文气象时间序列的模拟与重建，在法国东北部六个站点验证了其在数据缺失情况下进行预测和插补的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文气象时间序列观测数据有限且高度可变，传统统计方法难以准确刻画其动力学特征，亟需更强大的深度学习方法提升建模能力。

Method: 采用Transformer架构的扩散模型，对法国东北部六个站点的水量和水质数据进行联合建模，利用超过15年的质控观测数据进行校准与验证，并与多种基线方法对比。

Result: 所提出的Transformer扩散模型在时间序列插补和预测两个任务上均优于传统基线方法，能够有效捕捉和模拟水文数据的复杂时序模式。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够在观测数据存在不同程度缺失的情况下高效采样真实的时间序列分布，为水文气象建模提供了有前景的新工具。

Abstract: The modeling of hydrometeorological time series with limited observations is a key challenge in the monitoring of hydro-systems and water resources, as well as for flood or drought risk assessment. Due to the high variability of the underlying processes and the sparsity of available measurements, traditional statistical approaches often struggle to accurately represent their dynamics. In this context, recent advances in deep learning offer a promising direction for improving the representation and generation of complex temporal processes sampled at several observation sites. This study investigates the application of transformer-based diffusion models to the simulation and reconstruction of hydrological time series. The proposed framework is applied to the joint modeling of water quantity and quality at six sites spread across three adjacent headwater catchments located in North-East France on a limestone plateau covered by forests and field crops. The model is calibrated and validated using available observational data, which has been quality controlled and corrected for sensor drift and malfunction through collaborative efforts by LNE metrology expertise and Andra monthly quality control over more than 15 years. Its performance is compared with several established baseline approaches commonly used for time series modeling. Quantitative evaluation metrics are employed to assess the ability of the proposed method to reproduce key temporal characteristics of the observed signals in two settings: the imputation of incomplete time series and the forecasting of upcoming hydrological conditions.  Results support the effectiveness of the transformer-based approach and highlight its capacity to capture and simulate the complex patterns present in hydrological data. In particular, the results indicate that diffusion models can efficiently sample realistic time series distributions under observation settings with variable missing data for both forecasting and imputation.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [Human-Aware Power Restoration for Fair Outage Experience in Distribution Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20580)
*Majid Dehghani,Arastoo H Salimi,Hamidreza Nazaripouya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑客户停电体验的、面向公平性的人本化配电网恢复方法，通过概率故障建模、空间自适应分区策略和分布式维修调度算法，在随机停电条件下实现高效且公平的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统配电网恢复方法将故障视为确定性事件，忽略了停电事件的随机性以及不同客户的差异化停电体验，缺乏对公平性和人本化因素的考量。

Method: 提出人本化公平恢复框架：1）基于概率故障建模引导资源分配；2）空间自适应分区策略平衡各区恢复时间；3）自适应分布式维修调度算法加速恢复；4）采用滚动时域优化动态最小化总恢复时间。

Result: 在改进的69节点配电系统上的随机故障仿真结果表明，该模型能有效实现社会公平且考虑客户感知的恢复效果。

Conclusion: 该方法能够兼顾恢复速度与客户感知公平性，为随机停电场景下的配电网恢复提供了更符合实际需求的人本化方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel methodology for human-aware and fair service restoration in power distribution networks, explicitly accounting for the customer experience of outage duration. The complexity of this problem stems from the inherently unpredictable and stochastic nature of power outage events. Traditional approaches often oversimplify the problem by treating failures as deterministic, overlooking the lived experiences of customers and the true uncertainty of outage patterns. In contrast, the proposed method incorporates the probability of potential failures to guide a customer-aware and fairness-driven resource allocation, ensuring that restoration is not only fast but also perceived as fair from the customer's perspective. To achieve this, a spatially distributed, adaptive, and scalable partitioning policy is designed to balance restoration time across all failure locations, promoting consistency and equity in the outage experience. Next, an adaptive and distributed repair crew dispatch algorithm is proposed to accelerate service restoration while ensuring that no customer segment is disproportionately affected. The framework leverages a Receding Horizon (RH) optimization algorithm to dynamically minimize total restoration time amid randomly occurring outages in both space and time. Simulation results on modified 69-bus distribution networks under stochastic outage conditions demonstrate the model's effectiveness in delivering socially fair and customer-sensitive restoration outcomes

</details>


### [316] [Robust Adaptive Backup Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20842)
*Ersin Daş,David E. J. van Wijk,Tamas G. Molnar,Aaron D. Ames,Joel W. Burdick*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对存在参数不确定性的非线性控制系统，提出了一种鲁棒自适应备份控制障碍函数方法，通过自适应估计器提供参数估计误差边界，并采用对偶重构成处理执行矩阵不确定性，最终在输入约束下保证系统安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统备份控制障碍函数依赖精确模型预测轨迹，当系统存在未知参数时预测不准确，导致安全保障失效。现有方法难以同时处理漂移动力学和执行矩阵中的参数不确定性。

Method: 1) 使用逐元素认证自适应估计器提供参数自适应律和分量级估计误差界；2) 利用估计模型计算备份流，并用认证边界收紧安全条件；3) 针对执行矩阵不确定性，采用对偶重构建模为二次规划安全滤波器。

Result: 证明了满足所提鲁棒自适应备份控制障碍函数约束的控制器，在参数不确定性和输入约束下能够保证系统安全。

Conclusion: 该方法有效扩展了备份CBF到含参数不确定性的非线性系统，为实际工程中模型不精确情况下的安全控制提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We propose a notion of robust adaptive backup control barrier functions for nonlinear control affine systems with parametric uncertainty in both the drift dynamics and actuation matrix. Backup control barrier functions guarantee safety by predicting the system's trajectory under a pre-certified safe controller. However, these predictions rely on the model and can be inaccurate when the system contains unknown parameters. To address this issue, we estimate the unknown parameters using element-wise certified adaptive estimators that provide a parameter adaptation law and component-wise estimation error bounds. We compute the backup flow using the estimated model and tighten the safety conditions using these certified bounds. The resulting safety conditions account for the sensitivity of the predicted flow to parameter estimation errors. Moreover, to handle uncertainty in the actuation matrix, we use a duality-based reformulation that enables the use of a computationally efficient quadratic-program-based safety filter. We prove that controllers satisfying the proposed robust adaptive backup control barrier function constraints guarantee safety under parametric uncertainty and input constraints.

</details>


### [317] [Interaction Dynamics Modeling and Predictive Control for Safe Steerable Catheter--Tissue Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20939)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对导管-组织交互动力学问题，提出一种结合部分物理前馈、预测优化器与增广卡尔曼滤波的控制框架。通过显式约束接触力，实现了在满足0.5N安全力边界下的精确跟踪，在仿真中将自由空间逼近误差降低90%，约束控制器将接触力控制在0.47N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导管安全控制本质是交互动力学问题：尖端需跟踪规划运动、对软组织保持柔顺、抑制摩擦与迟滞，同时严格遵守临床有意义的接触力上限。现有方法难以同时兼顾跟踪精度与力安全。

Method: 1) 部分物理前馈消除可靠的标称弯曲动力学，暴露出配置不变的线性交互动力学模型；2) 预测优化器在接触力、腱力、曲率硬约束下调控交互状态；3) 增广卡尔曼滤波器将接触、摩擦、建模误差压缩为无传感器的扰动状态，实现自由空间无偏调节，同时将力安全留给显式约束。

Result: 在MuJoCo八连杆腱驱动导管仿真中：扰动增广削减自由空间逼近误差90%；仅有力约束的预测交互动力学控制器能在0.5N力边界下实现跟踪（约束控制器0.47N vs 无约束0.60N）；该边界在0.5mm、1.2Hz心脏运动下依然有效。

Conclusion: 无偏运动调节与接触力安全是耦合的交互动力学目标，显式预测约束能在刚性组织接触下解决二者的冲突。硬件验证留作未来工作。

Abstract: Safe steerable catheter control is fundamentally a problem of interaction dynamics: the tip must follow a planned motion, remain compliant against moving tissue, reject friction and hysteresis, and respect a clinically meaningful never-exceed contact-force bound. We formulate catheter--tissue interaction dynamics in the scalar tip-normal coordinate of a single-segment single-tendon catheter. A partial-physics feedforward cancels only the reliable nominal bending dynamics, exposing a configuration-invariant linear interaction-dynamics model whose input gain varies through the scalar catheter inertia. A predictive optimizer then regulates this interaction state subject to hard contact-force, tendon-force, and curvature constraints. An augmented Kalman filter compresses contact, friction, and modeling error into one sensor-free disturbance state, giving nominal offset-free regulation in free space while leaving force safety to the explicit constraint. The unconstrained and disturbance-free limit recovers classical catheter impedance as a special realization of the same interaction dynamics, rather than as the main design object. In a MuJoCo distributed-compliance simulation of an eight-link tendon-driven catheter, disturbance augmentation cuts free-space approach error by 90\%, and only the force-constrained predictive interaction-dynamics controller reconciles tracking with the 0.5\,N bound: the unconstrained controller drives contact force to 0.60\,N against a penetrating target, while the constrained one holds 0.47\,N at identical tracking. These results show that offset-free motion regulation and contact-force safety are coupled interaction-dynamics objectives, and that the explicit predictive constraint resolves their tension under stiff tissue contact. The bound also holds under $0.5$\,mm, $1.2$\,Hz cardiac motion. Hardware validation is future work.

</details>


### [318] [Supervisory Control with Event Forcing Under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21040)
*Yin Tong,Kai Cai*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分观测下具有事件强制机制的离散事件系统监督控制，提出了强制一致性概念作为存在满足给定规范的监督者的充要条件，并证明了其性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督控制依赖事件的使能与禁用，但在引入强制机制和部分观测后，监督者的强制决策必须在所有不可区分串上保持一致，同时确保通过抢占排除所有违规转移，现有理论尚不完善。

Method: 引入强制一致性（forcing consistency）概念，并证明其是存在满足给定规范的监督者的充要条件；通过理论分析比较其与已有 forcibility 概念的关系。

Result: 证明强制一致性严格强于 forcibility（即 forcibility 不是充分条件），且强制一致性在并运算下不封闭（unlike forcibility）。

Conclusion: 这些结果为同时存在强制机制和观测限制的监督者综合奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we address supervisory control in discrete-event systems with event forcing. In particular, partial observation of the supervisor is considered. Unlike traditional supervisory control, which relies on event enablement and disablement, the forcing mechanism allows a supervisor to preempt unwanted transitions by actively triggering forcible events. Under partial observation, we face a new challenge where the supervisor's forcing decisions must be consistent across all indistinguishable strings meanwhile guarantee all violating transitions are excluded through preemption. We introduce the concept of forcing consistency as a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of a supervisor that achieves a given specification. We prove that forcing consistency is strictly stronger than the notion of forcibility but, unlike the latter, is not closed under union. These results lay the theoretical foundation for supervisor synthesis in the presence of both forcing mechanisms and observation limitations.

</details>


### [319] [A scalable and resource-efficient pipelined p-computer for probabilistic Ising machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21077)
*Deborah Volpe,Eleonora Raimondo,Andrea Grimaldi,Pedram Khalili Amiri,Stefano Chiappini,Anna Giordano,Mario Carpentieri,Hyunsoo Yang,Massimo Chiappini,Giovanni Finocchio*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流水线架构的FPGA实现，用于全连接概率伊辛机（PIM），通过深度流水线化（>20级）概率比特更新路径和带宽感知的片上存储组织，在512/1024/2048 p-bit规模下实现高达300MHz的运行频率，相比非流水线基线方案在更新速率上提升一个数量级，并显著缩短了密集优化问题的求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字概率伊辛机虽可通过稀疏交互实现高吞吐，但难以处理密集组合优化问题，因为内存带宽和数据移动成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 采用深度流水线化（>20级）的概率比特更新路径，重叠自旋评估与局部场更新；结合带宽感知的片上存储组织来管理耦合矩阵和偏置矩阵；支持不同精度（16/10/2-bit）和规模（512/1024/2048 p-bit）的可扩展模块化设计。

Result: 在固定并行度下，更新速率比优化后的非流水线基线提升一个数量级，改进了密集工作负载的时间-面积权衡；在投资组合优化和LDPC解码任务上，与软件参考高度一致，且求解时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 流水线架构是解决密集优化问题中可扩展数字概率计算的有效途径。

Abstract: Probabilistic Ising machines (PIMs) based on probabilistic bits offer a hardware-friendly route to solve combinatorial optimization problems, but most digital implementations achieve high throughput by exploiting sparse interactions. This limits their applicability to dense problems, for which memory bandwidth and data movement become the dominant bottlenecks. Here, we show a resource-efficient pipelined Field-Programmable Gate Array architecture enabling high-throughput execution of fully-connected PIMs while maintaining scalability and modularity. This architecture design combines a deeply pipelined (>20 stages) probabilistic bit update path, which overlaps spin evaluation and local-field updates, with a bandwidth-aware on-chip memory organization for the coupling and bias matrices. The architecture supports 512 p-bits with 16-bit fixed-point coefficients and 1024 and 2048 p-bits with 10-bit and 2-bit coefficients, respectively, and operates at up to 300 MHz. At fixed degree of parallelization, it delivers an order-of-magnitude higher update rate than an optimized non-pipelined baseline, while improving the time-area trade-off for dense workloads. Validation on portfolio optimization and low-density parity-check decoding shows close agreement with software references and substantial reductions in time-to-solution relative to the non-pipelined design, establishing pipelining as an effective route to scalable digital probabilistic computing for dense optimization problems.

</details>


### [320] [Certified Stochastic Control via Covariance Steering with Pick-to-Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21086)
*Chun-Wei Kong,Zachary Donovan,Morteza Lahijanian,Jay McMahon*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: CS-P2L将协方差控制(CS)与Pick-to-Learn(P2L)元算法相结合，用于在高保真随机模拟器上进行认证控制器合成，通过迭代策略评估和压缩式概率保证，在航天器动力下降问题上实现了比独立CS更准确的违规率认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协方差控制方法在高保真随机模拟器上会低估违规率（约低估一半），需要一种能够提供严格概率保证的认证控制器合成框架。

Method: 将协方差控制(CS)与Pick-to-Learn(P2L)元算法耦合，迭代地在模拟器滚动中评估策略，使用最坏违规情况收紧替代约束，并基于压缩理论在给定置信水平下提供真实违规概率的保证。

Result: 在重力不确定的航天器动力下降问题中，CS-P2L仅用600次滚动即可认证4.9%的违规界限，而独立协方差控制将违规率低估了约两倍。

Conclusion: CS-P2L框架能够有效地为高保真随机模拟器提供更准确、有严格概率保证的控制器认证。

Abstract: We present CS-P2L, a framework coupling covariance steering (CS) with the Pick-to-Learn (P2L) meta-algorithm for certified controller synthesis over high-fidelity stochastic simulators. The method iteratively evaluates policies on simulator rollouts, tightens surrogate constraints using the worst-case violations, and provides compression-based probabilistic guarantees on the true violation probability given a confidence level. On a spacecraft powered-descent problem with uncertain gravity, CS-P2L certifies a violation bound of 4.9\% with 600 rollouts, whereas standalone covariance steering underestimates the violation rate by roughly a factor of two.

</details>


### [321] [Semi-Explicit Solutions to the Prying-Pedestrian Surveillance-Evasion Differential Game and Extensions to Two Pursuers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21087)
*Philipp Braun,Daniel Coutinho,Timothy L. Molloy,Iman Shames*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从1v1追逃博弈扩展到2v1（双追捕者vs单逃逸者）场景，通过几何重解释和半显式解，针对静态追捕者和逃逸者速度至少为追捕者两倍的情况给出部分解，并提出了避免坐标变换的新求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的1v1监视-逃逸微分博弈扩展到2v1场景，探究多追捕者协同下的优势，同时克服原方法（坐标变换）在更高维问题中的局限性。

Method: 1) 对1v1结果进行半显式或几何重解释；2) 基于这些重解释，推导2v1博弈的部分解；3) 针对静态追捕者和速度比≥2的逃逸者两种情况分别求解；4) 提出新的求解方法，避免原1v1中将博弈降至二维的坐标变换。

Result: 获得了2v1博弈的部分解（静态追捕者场景和速度比≥2场景），且新方法不仅解了2v1问题，还给出了1v1最优策略的新几何解释。

Conclusion: 新方法为2v1博弈提供了可行进展，同时深化了对1v1博弈策略的几何理解，并为进一步扩展（如更多追捕者或不同速度比）打开了可能性。

Abstract: In [1], the authors recently proposed and solved a surveillance-evasion differential game in which an agile pursuer (the prying pedestrian) seeks to remain within a given surveillance range of a less agile evader for as long as possible while the evader seeks to escape as quickly as possible. In this paper, we provide initial results that extend this game from the 1 versus 1 (1v1) setting to a 2 versus 1 (2v1) setting with two pursuers and one evader. By deriving and exploiting semi-explicit or geometric reinterpretations of the existing 1v1 results, we derive partial solutions to the 2v1 game for the case of static pursuers and for the case of an evader that is at least twice as fast as the pursuers. While the 2v1 results of this paper build on the 1v1 results of [1], a different solution approach is developed to avoid a coordinate transformation that reduces the 1v1 game to two dimensions but which is ineffective at simplifying the 2v1 game. Beyond enabling progress on the 2v1 game, our new approach yields new geometric interpretations of the optimal pursuer and evader strategies in the 1v1 game, and opens further possible extensions.

</details>


### [322] [Safety and Security: Experimental Validation of Encrypted Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21136)
*Juraj Holaza,Martin Kalúz,Matúš Furka,Martin Klaučo,Juraj Oravec*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多项式近似最优控制律的方法，在完全同态加密框架内评估显式控制律，实现了加密模型预测控制（MPC）的安全部署，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全优化控制方法几乎不存在，且部分实现无法解决约束MPC的闭环系统稳定性和递归可行性问题。亟需一种既能保护数据与控制器系数，又能保证系统稳定性的安全MPC方案。

Method: 采用多项式近似最优控制律，并在完全同态加密框架下评估该显式控制律，从而在保护过程数据和控制器系数的前提下，安全地将控制器部署在第三方或云端平台。

Result: 通过实验室规模的实际部署与验证，证明了所提出的隐私感知控制方法具有优势。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了加密MPC的稳定性和递归可行性问题，为安全过程控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the problem of an encrypted model predictive control (MPC) design, representing a significant challenge in the recent field of secure process control. Existing methods in secure optimization-based control are non-existent and even partial implementation fails to address the closed-loop system stability and recursive feasibility properties of the constrained MPC. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel approach that utilizes a polynomial approximation of the optimal control law. This method evaluates the explicit control law within a fully homomorphic encryption framework, ensuring that the controller is securely deployed on any third-party or cloud-based platform, with both process data and controller coefficients protected. Experimental results from a laboratory-scale implementation and validation of the proposed privacy-aware control method demonstrate its advantages.

</details>


### [323] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Gain Tuning in Control of Teleoperation Manipulators with Joint Flexibility and Time-Varying Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21145)
*Armin Attarzadeh,Mohammad Ali Ghaemifar,Alireza Khanzadeh,Soheil Ganjefar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对存在关节柔性和时变通信延迟的双边遥操作系统，提出了一种结合P+d控制器与TD3深度强化学习的混合控制方法，在保证稳定性的同时减少振动并提高跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节柔性和时变通信延迟同时存在时，传统控制方法难以维持主从机器人的稳定和协调运动，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 混合控制方法：P+d控制器提供基础稳定性保障，TD3深度强化学习智能体实时调整远程侧的比例和阻尼增益，Lyapunov-Krasovskii分析保证时变延迟下的稳定性。

Result: 在有界时变延迟下系统稳定性有理论保证，且能有效减少振动、改善跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为同时面临关节柔性和不确定网络延迟的遥操作系统提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Bilateral teleoperation systems that include joint flexibility better reflect real robotic systems used in surgery, space, and rehabilitation. However, joint flexibility together with time-varying communication delays makes it difficult to maintain stable and coordinated motion between the master and slave robots. To address this, we propose a hybrid control method that combines a stable Proportional-plus-Damping (P+d) controller with a model-free deep reinforcement learning agent based on the Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) algorithm. The P+d controller provides basic stability under bounded delays, while the learning agent adjusts and tunes the remote-side proportional and damping gains in real time to reduce vibrations and improve tracking. Stability is guaranteed for bounded time-varying delays using Lyapunov-Krasovskii analysis. The approach provides a practical solution for teleoperation systems facing both joint flexibility and uncertain network delays.

</details>


### [324] [When Persistency is not Exciting in Data-Driven Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21280)
*Gianluca Giacomelli,Chuyu Lu,Siep Weiland,Valentina Breschi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文指出，在数据驱动控制中，仅满足秩条件不足以确保良好的闭环性能，数据还需激发控制目标关注的频率成分，这为面向控制的实验设计奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制方法主要依赖秩条件来评估数据质量，但作者发现仅满足秩条件并不总能保证满意的闭环性能，因此需要更深入理解数据对控制目标的意义。

Method: 作者聚焦于无法使用白噪声激励的数据采集场景，从频域角度分析线性行为表示的隐含条件，论证数据需同时满足秩条件和激发控制目标关注的频率成分。

Result: 数值实验表明，仅满足秩条件但忽略跟踪控制目标的数据，会导致数据驱动的预测控制器在闭环系统中无法跟踪所选参考信号。

Conclusion: 数据必须同时满足秩条件和频率激发要求，这为面向直接数据驱动方法的控制导向实验设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Understanding how to collect data that is meaningful for control purposes is of paramount importance in data-driven control. While existing approaches have primarily relied on the satisfaction of a rank condition to assess the quality of an experiment, we show that satisfying it is not always sufficient to achieve satisfactory closed-loop performance. Focusing on scenarios where white-noise-like excitation cannot be used for data collection, we examine the frequency-domain implications of linear behavioral representation. This analysis demonstrates that data must both satisfy the rank condition and excite the frequencies of interest for the control goal, thereby laying the foundations for control-oriented experiment design tailored to direct data-driven approaches. These findings are reflected in our numerical results. Data-enabled predictive controllers that rely on data satisfying the rank condition but neglect the tracking control goal result in a closed-loop system that cannot track the selected reference.

</details>


### [325] [Interoperability of Electric Drive Models using HELICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21486)
*Ajay Pratap Yadav*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于HELICS的协同仿真框架，解决电机系统建模中因编程语言和组件分离导致的互操作性问题，通过永磁同步电机驱动系统的协同仿真验证了其减少开发时间、灵活复用模型及高效集成到软件和硬件在环环境的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电机系统模型因编程语言（C、MATLAB、Python）差异及逆变器、控制器等组件分离于不同环境，面临互操作性挑战；同时HIL等先进平台要求反复适配，亟需统一协调框架。

Method: 采用HELICS作为协同仿真主干，连接异构工具和模型，实现不同编程语言和仿真环境下的电机驱动模型无缝协调与数据交换。

Result: 在速度控制下的逆变器馈电永磁同步电机驱动系统中成功实现协同仿真，验证了开发时间缩短、现有模型灵活复用以及软件/HIL环境的便捷集成。

Conclusion: 该框架为电机驱动系统测试提供了可扩展、模块化的协同解决方案，适用于协作式与可重复的开发流程。

Abstract: Accurate modeling and simulation are essential for the effective design, testing, and evaluation of electric machine systems. However, existing models often face interoperability challenges due to differences in programming languages (e.g., C, MATLAB, Python) and the separation of components such as inverters and controllers across diverse environments. These challenges are amplified by the growing use of advanced simulation platforms like Hardware-in-the-Loop (HIL) and Controller-HIL, which require repeated adaptations for compatibility. This paper presents a HELICS-based co-simulation framework that enables seamless coordination among heterogeneous tools and models, providing a unified platform for integrating and testing electric drive models regardless of their origin. The approach is demonstrated through the co-simulation of an inverter-fed permanent magnet synchronous machine (PMSM) drive under speed control, showcasing reduced development time, flexible reuse of existing models, and efficient integration into both software and HIL environments offering a scalable, modular solution for collaborative and repeatable electric drive system testing and development.

</details>


### [326] [Through-the-Earth Magnetic Induction Communication and Networking: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14854)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Wei Ni,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Yongbin Gao,Dusit Niyato,Ekram Hossain*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了磁感应通信（MIC）在地球穿透（TTE）场景下的研究进展，提出了新的信道分解模型和几何模型用于分析MI快衰落，并设计了一套支持TCP/IP和Linux的MIC框架以加速其在空天地海一体化网络中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI通信虽具有优异的穿透能力，但其快衰落现象尚处于研究早期，且缺乏统一的网络架构支持其与空天地海一体化网络的融合。

Method: 1) 对MI信道功率增益进行细粒度分解为四种物理参数；2) 提出新型几何模型分析MI快衰落；3) 总结中继技术和串扰效应；4) 提出支持TCP/IP和Linux的MIC框架。

Result: 建立了MI信道的四参数分解模型；提出了MI快衰落的几何分析模型；设计了可集成现有及新兴MIC解决方案的完整框架。

Conclusion: MI通信在穿透地球场景中具有巨大潜力，但仍面临快衰落建模、网络协议设计等挑战，所提框架可加速深度学习等前沿技术在MIC中的应用。

Abstract: Magnetic induction (MI) communication (MIC) has emerged as a promising candidate for underground communication networks due to its excellent penetration capabilities. Integration with Space-Air-Ground-Underground (SAGUI) networks in next-generation mobile communication systems requires a well-defined network architecture. A recent discovery in MIC research, MI fast fading, remains in its early stages and presents unique challenges. This paper provides a comprehensive survey on through-the-earth (TTE) MIC, covering MI applications, channel modeling, point-to-point MIC design, relay techniques, network frameworks, and emerging technologies. We compare various MIC applications to highlight TTE-specific challenges and review the principles of channel modeling, addressing both MI slow fading and MI fast fading, along with its potential impact on existing MIC theories. We conduct a fine-grained decomposition of MI channel power gain into four distinct physical parameters, and propose a novel geometric model to analyze MI fast fading. We also summarize MI relay techniques, examine crosstalk effects in relay and high-density networks, and explore key research tasks within the OSI framework for a holistic MI network protocol in SAGUI. To bridge the gaps identified, we propose a MIC framework that supports TCP/IP and Linux, enabling full implementation of existing and emerging MIC solutions. This framework empowers researchers to leverage Linux resources and deep learning platforms for accelerated development of MIC in SAGUI networks. Remaining research challenges, open issues, and promising novel techniques are further identified to advance MIC research.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Using Pre-Trends for Inference in Difference-in-Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21312)
*Clément de Chaisemartin*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预处理期趋势作为参考分布的DID推断方法，无需平行趋势假设，适用于多期预处理数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DID方法依赖平行趋势假设，但在多期预处理数据中，观察到的预处理趋势本身包含关于后处理期可能发生冲击的信息，现有方法未能充分利用这一信息。

Method: 利用预处理期的结果演化作为后处理期DID估计量的参考分布，构建类似于保形推断的程序，但使用DID特定的预测器，从而放弃平行趋势假设。

Result: 该程序在不需要平行趋势假设的情况下，实现了对处理效应的有效推断。

Conclusion: 提出了一种更灵活的DID推断框架，放松了平行趋势假设，适用于具有多期预处理数据的场景，与现有保形推断方法相比具有独特的识别约束。

Abstract: Difference-in-differences (DID) are sometimes estimated with many pre-treatment periods. In such settings, the observed pre-treatment outcome evolutions provide direct information about the magnitude of shocks that could also occur after treatment. This paper proposes a simple inference procedure that uses those pre-trends as the reference distribution for the post-treatment DID. The procedure is closely related to existing conformal inference procedures, but its DID-specific predictor leads to a distinct identifying restriction. Existing procedures assume parallel trends, while this paper's procedure does not require parallel trends.

</details>


### [328] [Uniformly Consistent Semi-nonparametric Demand Estimation with Micro-Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21323)
*Richard Grigorian*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文针对半非参数差异化产品需求模型，提出了一种基于微观选择数据的剖面筛子最小距离估计量，并证明了当市场数量和最小市场内样本量同时增长时，该估计量的一致性和渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差异化产品需求模型在利用微观选择数据时，面临消费者异质性函数和价格侧函数灵活估计的挑战，且市场截距项数量随市场数量增长带来统计推断困难。

Method: 基于Berry and Haile (2024)框架，利用市场内消费者协变量变化恢复灵活的消费者异质性函数和市场特定复合截距；再通过排除价格工具变量将截距分解为灵活价格侧函数和结构需求冲击；采用剖面筛子最小距离估计方法处理随市场数量增长的截距项。

Result: 当市场数量和最小市场内样本量同时增长时，剖面步骤渐近可忽略，公共结构函数一致且均匀一致地得到估计；蒙特卡洛模拟验证了一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了微观数据下半非参数需求模型的估计问题，灵活的价格侧估计对反事实需求分析具有重要价值。

Abstract: This paper develops a profiled sieve minimum-distance estimator for a semi-nonparametric differentiated-products demand model with micro-level choice data. Building on Berry and Haile (2024), the estimator uses within-market variation in consumer covariates to recover a flexible consumer-heterogeneity function and market-specific composite intercepts. Excluded price instruments then separate these intercepts into a flexible price-side function and structural demand shocks. The main statistical challenge is that the number of profiled market intercepts grows with the number of markets. I show that, when both the number of markets and the minimum within-market sample size grow, this profiling step is asymptotically negligible and the common structural functions are uniformly consistently estimated. Monte Carlo evidence supports the consistency result and illustrates the value of flexible price-side estimation for counterfactual demand analysis.

</details>


### [329] [Stochastic Potential Choices and Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21413)
*Aureo de Paula,Elie Tamer*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文探讨当个体层面的结果和治疗分配具有随机性时，传统工具变量（IV）估计量的解释会如何变化。在随机模型下，IV估计量不再仅识别“依从者”的因果效应，而是对所有个体的平均处理效应进行加权，权重取决于工具变量改变其治疗概率的程度。论文还基于信息为随机选择提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计量经济学模型假设个体在治疗和对照下的结果是固定的，工具变量下治疗决策也是固定的。但现实中个体的结果和治疗选择可能具有随机性，现有理论缺乏对这种随机性的严格刻画，导致对估计量解释可能产生偏差。

Method: 论文建立了一个随机潜在结果模型：每个个体具有一个稳定的（但可能是随机的）响应类型，包含每个状态下的治疗选择概率，以及每个治疗-状态对下的潜在结果分布。通过比较确定性IV模型和随机模型，分析估计量解释的变化。

Result: 在确定性IV模型中，估计量只识别“依从者”的处理效应；而在随机治疗分配或选择下，不存在这样的子群体。估计量平均了所有个体的效应，权重为工具变量改变其治疗概率的程度。论文还给出了基于信息的随机选择理论基础。

Conclusion: 随机化个体层面的结果和治疗选择改变了IV估计量的解释，使其从仅识别依从者效应变为对所有个体效应的加权平均，这要求研究者在应用时重新审视估计量的含义。

Abstract: Applied econometricians typically model each individual as having fixed outcomes under treatment and control and, in instrumental-variables (IV) settings, fixed treatment decisions under each value of the instrument. This paper asks what changes when outcomes and treatment allocations or choices are stochastic at the individual level. In the model, each individual has a stable (but possibly stochastic) response type consisting of two objects: a treatment choice probability under each state and a potential outcome distribution under each treatment-state pair. These stochastic potential outcomes change the interpretation of some familiar estimators. For instance, in the deterministic IV model, the estimand identifies treatment effect only for compliers - those whose treatment status switches with the instrument. Under stochastic treatment allocation or choice there is no such subgroup: the estimand averages effects over all individuals, weighting each by how much the instrument, policy, or assignment rule moves their probability of treatment. The paper then gives an information-based foundation for stochastic choice, in which individuals act on expected gains given their information.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [First-Order Analysis of Optimization in Uniformly Convex Metric Spaces: Directional Subderivatives and Basic Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20677)
*D. Russell Luke,Titus Pinta,Qinyu Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为一致凸度量空间中的显式一阶方法开发了分析工具，提出了基于方向次导数、函数值和迭代的收敛充分条件，并证明了最速下降方向的存在性，为非凸优化提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于一致凸度量空间中的一阶优化方法缺乏系统的收敛性分析工具，特别是针对非凸光滑优化的理论基础尚不完善。

Method: 通过引入方向次导数、函数值和迭代序列的收敛条件，结合有界性、测地线光滑性和度量Polyak-Łojasiewicz性质等正则性假设，建立了统一的收敛性分析框架，并证明了最速下降方向的存在性。

Result: 推导出了在一致凸度量空间中显式一阶方法收敛的充分条件；证明了满足收敛假设的最速下降方向的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作为局部光滑非凸优化的显式一阶算法奠定了理论基础，拓展了优化理论在度量空间中的应用范围。

Abstract: We develop tools for the analysis and implementation of explicit first-order methods for minimizing functions in uniformly convex metric spaces. We formulate sufficient conditions for convergence of descent sequences in terms of directional subderivatives, function values and iterates under regularity assumptions including boundedness, geodesic smoothness and a metric Polyak-Łojasiewicz property. We show that there exists a steepest descent direction in which the assumptions for convergence are satisfied.
