This work provides a foundation for first-order explicit algorithms for locally smooth, nonconvex optimization.

</details>


### [331] [Rule-Induced Behavior of Fuzzy Scalar Objective Functions for Reliable Multi-Criteria Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20731)
*Olaf Frommann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了模糊规则标量目标函数在多准则优化中的行为，发现成员函数位置、隐式单准则基线和显式规则后件会显著影响其优化行为。研究表明，表面上的参考点跟踪可能由平坦的规则激活平台导致，而真正的参考点跟踪需要具有低歧义性的局部极小值。在受控参考设置中，基于参考的三类规则集能够一致地改进受控参考、恢复Pareto集，并避免平台驱动的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊规则标量目标函数能够灵活编码定性偏好和参考区域，但其诱导的标量偏好景观可能与预期的决策语义存在显著差异，需要系统研究这种不一致性及其成因。

Method: 采用两个解析控制的双准则Pareto前沿和一个嵌入的二维受控参考公式，通过分离前沿选择机制与参考改进行为，分析成员函数位置、隐式基线规则和显式规则后件对模糊标量目标函数行为的影响。

Result: 表面参考跟踪可由平坦规则激活平台引起；真正参考跟踪需要低歧义性局部极小值；全局成员函数与竞争后件能恢复鲁棒Pareto权衡但不一定改进每个参考设计；基于参考的三类规则集能一致改进受控参考、恢复Pareto集并避免平台驱动选择。

Conclusion: 研究提供了诊断度量和实用指导，用于构建优化行为与预期决策语义一致的模糊标量化函数。

Abstract: Fuzzy-rule-based scalar objective functions provide a flexible way to encode qualitative preferences, reference regions, and interactions between criteria in multi-criteria optimization and decision making. However, the scalar preference landscape induced by such rules can differ substantially from the intended decision semantics. This paper investigates how membership placement, implicit single-criterion baseline rules, and explicit rule consequents affect the behavior of fuzzy scalar objective functions. Two analytically controlled bi-criteria Pareto fronts and an embedded two-dimensional dominated-reference formulation are used to separate front-selection mechanisms from reference improvement behavior. The study shows that apparent reference following can be caused by flat rule-activation plateaus, whereas genuine reference following requires localized minima with low tie ambiguity. In the dominated-reference setting, global memberships with competing consequents recover robust Pareto tradeoffs but do not necessarily improve each reference design. By contrast, a reference-based three-class rule set consistently improves dominated references, recovers the Pareto set, and avoids plateau-driven selection in the present tests. The results provide diagnostic metrics and practical guidance for constructing fuzzy scalarizations whose optimization behavior is consistent with the intended decision semantics.

</details>


### [332] [A Safeguarded Projected-Gradient Framework for Complementarity-Constrained Least-Squares Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20786)
*Lianghai Xiao,Wei Zhang,Jiayi Zhong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个安全的投影梯度框架，统一求解广义绝对值方程和线性互补问题，并分析收敛性，数值实验验证了高精度和计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAVE和LCP广泛出现在均衡和优化模型中，两者都可转化为互补集上的最小二乘问题，需要统一高效的求解框架。

Method: 基于互补集上的最小二乘形式，提出安全投影梯度框架，针对两类问题设计不同的代数精化策略，并分析收敛性及有限步恢复唯一解的条件。

Result: 在GAVE和LCP基准测试中，框架达到高最终精度，并展现了问题依赖的计算权衡。

Conclusion: 安全投影梯度框架能有效统一求解GAVE和LCP，收敛性有理论保证，数值表现良好。

Abstract: Generalized absolute value equations (GAVEs) and linear complementarity problems (LCPs) arise in many equilibrium and optimization models and can both be formulated as least-squares problems over the complementarity set. Building on this shared formulation, we propose a safeguarded projected-gradient framework for both classes with problem-dependent algebraic refinements. We analyze its convergence properties and, under stronger conditions, establish finite recovery of the unique solution. Numerical experiments on GAVE and LCP benchmarks illustrate the practical behavior of the framework, including its high final accuracy in the tested settings and its problem-dependent computational tradeoffs.

</details>


### [333] [Mean field and N-agent games for optimal relative consumption-investment with jump risk and common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20845)
*Yiming Jiang,Fuxing Li,Yawei Wei,Zimeng Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了N人博弈中的最优消费-投资问题及其均值场博弈，在相对业绩担忧下推导出确定性均值场均衡，并建立了近似纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Merton消费-投资问题扩展到竞争性多智能体场景，考虑相对业绩担忧（消费和终端财富两方面），以及向下跳跃风险、共同噪声和异质噪声的影响。

Method: 采用N人博弈及均值场博弈框架，运用随机最大值原理（Stochastic Maximum Principle）在均值场极限下解析地表征确定性均值场均衡，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 得到了均值场均衡的解析解形式，并基于此构造了N人博弈的近似纳什均衡，数值实验展示了均衡及其金融含义。

Conclusion: 本文为竞争性代理人之间的消费-投资决策提供了理论均衡框架，证明了均值场均衡可作为有限N人博弈的有效近似。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal consumption--investment problem for competitive agents in an \(N\)-player game and its associated mean field game. Each agent invests in an individual risky asset subject to idiosyncratic noise, common noise and downward jump risk, and the interaction among agents is induced by relative performance concerns in both consumption and terminal wealth. In the mean field limit, we characterize a deterministic mean field equilibrium in analytical form by using the stochastic maximum principle. Numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the resulting equilibrium and its financial implications. Finally, based on the obtained mean field equilibrium, we construct an approximate Nash equilibrium for the \(N\)-player game. This model is motivated by \cite{Merton1971} and \cite{Lacker2020}.

</details>


### [334] [Structure-Preserving Spectral Dynamic Programming on Compact Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20854)
*Shanqing Liu,Yang Qi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连通紧致李群上有限时域最优控制的动态规划半群的谱逼近方法，通过引入Fejér型滤波器替代正交投影，恢复了离散层面的动态规划结构，并证明了对于Lipschitz数据以O(√δ+√(ε+1/(δN²)))的速率收敛，其中δ为时间步长，N为谱分辨率，ε为粘性项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Galekin谱方法在紧致李群上求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程时，由于Bellman算子关于上确界范数单调非扩张，而Peter-Weyl分解下的正交投影在L²意义下最优，导致上确界范数误差被Lebesgue常数放大，在S¹上对数增长，在SO(3)上多项式增长，数值迭代会在几步内违反基本界，亟需恢复动态规划结构。

Method: 用Markov型谱滤波器替代正交投影，提出Fejér型滤波器（有限秩、保正性、非扩张）；引入辅助热核/消失粘性格式获取Lipschitz数据的定性上确界范数收敛；通过对滤波器进行随机扰动解释（小随机噪声扰动受控动力学）进行误差分析；扩展到基于正体积分的全离散实现，在SO(3)上实现精确Wigner传输。

Result: 对Lipschitz数据，证明了滤波器方案的收敛率O(√δ+√(ε+1/(δN²)))，粘性可为零，当δ=N⁻¹时收敛率N^{-1/2}；数值实验验证了预测的收敛率和滤波器偏差，量化了两种基于图的方法的帧依赖性。

Conclusion: 通过谱滤波器恢复动态规划结构的思路有效，无需价值函数先验正则性或粘性意义下的一致性论证，方法可推广到全离散实现。

Abstract: We study spectral approximations of the dynamic programming semigroup for finite-horizon optimal control on a connected compact Lie group $G$, and of the associated first-order Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. The Bellman operator is monotone and non-expansive in the supremum norm, while the Peter-Weyl decomposition of $L^{2}(G)$, on which every Fourier method on $G$ rests, is orthogonal, and the mismatch is quantitative. The natural sup-norm error recursion of the Galerkin iteration is amplified at every step by the Lebesgue constant of the spectral projection, which grows logarithmically on $S^{1}$ and polynomially on compact Lie groups of rank one, including $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and in computation the iteration violates elementary bounds within a few steps. We restore the dynamic programming structure at the discrete level by replacing the orthogonal projection with spectral filters of Markov type. An auxiliary heat-kernel/vanishing-viscosity scheme yields qualitative sup-norm convergence for Lipschitz data. The main result is a Fejér-type filter on $G$, finite-rank, positivity preserving and non-expansive, together with a convergence theorem at the rate $O(\sqrtδ+\sqrt{ε+1/(δN^2)})$ for Lipschitz data, where $δ$ is the time step, $N$ the spectral resolution and $ε$ the viscosity. The viscosity may be zero, and the coupling $δ=N^{-1}$ then gives the rate $N^{-1/2}$. The proof interprets the filter as a small random perturbation of the controlled dynamics, requires neither a priori regularity of the value function nor a consistency argument in the viscosity sense for the filtering step, and extends to a fully discrete realization based on positive cubature, with exact Wigner transport on $\mathrm{SO}(3)$. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates and filter bias and quantify the frame dependence of two chart-based baselines.

</details>


### [335] [Implicit Primal-Dual Guarantees in Unconstrained First-Order Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20875)
*Benjamin Grimmer,Alex L. Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明，在温和条件下，任何仅基于初始距离界保证原始目标间隙收敛的一阶方法，实际上都隐式地以相同速率保证了一个可计算的原始-对偶间隙，这揭示了对偶证书与动量方法中辅助序列的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一阶凸优化方法（特别是非光滑Lipschitz和光滑问题）设计收敛性证明，并揭示固定步长一阶方法中隐含的原始-对偶间隙保证。

Method: 基于性能估计问题（PEP）框架，证明任何保证原始目标间隙上界的一阶方法，实际上都隐式地提供了更强的原始-对偶间隙保证，这些对偶证书表现为仿射下界形式。

Result: 证明了在温和条件下，一阶方法对原始间隙的收敛保证自动蕴含了一个可计算的原始-对偶间隙保证，且收敛速率相同。

Conclusion: 隐式最优对偶证书（仿射下界）不仅提供了更强的收敛保证，还揭示了动量方法中辅助序列的作用机制。

Abstract: This work considers the design of first-order convex optimization algorithms and convergence proofs. In particular, we consider nonsmooth Lipschitz and smooth problems accessed through a subgradient or gradient oracle, respectively. For the general class of fixed-step first-order methods, prior work on Performance Estimation Problems (PEPs) has shown that structured, tight convergence proofs typically exist. Under mild conditions, we further show that any first-order method guaranteeing a bound on the primal objective gap $f(x_N)-f(x_\star)$ assuming only a bound on $\|x_0-x_\star\|$ actually has a stronger guarantee on an explicit, computable primal-dual gap at the same rate. These implicit optimal dual certificates, which take the form of affine lower bounds, also provide insight into the role of auxiliary sequences in momentum methods.

</details>


### [336] [Learning to recover: Adaptive local branching with reinforcement learning for log-truck routing and scheduling under disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20901)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,François Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的实时动态优化方法，用于解决加拿大林业中因道路关闭、车辆故障等突发事件导致的卡车路径与调度重新优化问题。通过构造以偏差参数k为约束的混合整数线性规划序列，并利用REINFORCE算法自适应选择k值，该方法在保持计划稳定性和运营成本之间生成帕累托前沿，实验表明其在恢复方案质量和计算效率上优于传统网格搜索和二分搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 林业物流中，预定的木材卡车调度计划常因道路关闭、车辆故障、运输延误和需求波动等突发干扰而失效。如何在极短时间内同时恢复计划可行性（满足当前运输需求）并尽可能减少与原计划的偏差（维持运营稳定性），是一个紧迫且具有挑战性的实际需求。

Method: 将恢复问题建模为一系列以偏差参数k（原计划与恢复计划之间的ℓ1距离）为约束的邻域受限混合整数线性规划；证明最小可行偏差值k_min可精确计算；采用REINFORCE策略梯度算法训练强化学习策略，根据求解器反馈自适应选择k值，以探索稳定-成本空间的帕累托前沿。

Result: 基于加拿大林业合作伙伴的历史数据，在涵盖所有类别的干扰场景下进行每周实例评估。该方法相比固定k值的网格搜索和二分搜索基线，能够以更少的求解器调用次数获得更丰富的帕累托前沿，且在各类配置和干扰类型中均能在运营可接受的重新优化时间内生成可行恢复计划。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习自适应k值选择策略有效平衡了恢复计划的可行性与稳定性，为调度员提供了结构化的决策选项集，在真实林业物流场景中展现了良好的实用性和效率优势。

Abstract: We consider the real-time reoptimisation of log-truck routing and scheduling in the Canadian forestry industry following unforeseen disruptions. Road closures, vehicle breakdowns, travel delays, and demand fluctuations invalidate pre-established tactical plans and call for recovery decisions that simultaneously restore feasibility and limit deviation from the original schedule, two objectives inherently in tension under tight time constraints. We formulate recovery as a sequence of neighbourhood-restricted mixed-integer linear programs parameterised by a deviation bound $k$, the $\ell_1$ distance between the recovered and baseline plans. We show that the minimum feasible value $k_{\min}$ can be computed exactly, anchoring the search at its most stable extreme. Rather than targeting a single $k$, we seek a Pareto front of non-dominated recovery plans spanning the stability--cost space, giving dispatchers a structured set of operational options. To navigate this space efficiently, we propose a reinforcement learning policy, trained with the REINFORCE policy-gradient algorithm, that adaptively selects successive values of $k$ from solver feedback, concentrating computational effort in productive regions and terminating exploration when further improvement is unlikely. Evaluated on weekly instances derived from historical data of a Canadian forestry partner, under disruption scenarios covering all event categories considered, the approach recovers richer Pareto fronts with fewer solver calls than fixed-$k$ grid and dichotomic search baselines, and produces feasible recovery plans within operationally acceptable reoptimization times across all configurations and disruption types.

</details>


### [337] [Boundary control and periodic trajectories of semilinear Euler equations with application to hydrogen transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20948)
*Alexander Zuyev,Peter Benner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用等温欧拉方程建立了受入口压力和出口质量流通量控制的管道气流数学模型，通过边界控制系统框架转化为非线性抽象微分方程，推导了傅里叶系数表示，建立了抽象空间中非线性控制系统周期解的存在条件，并应用于实际氢气输送模型以评估供需周期性波动下的周期性运行状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决管道气流在周期性供需波动下的运行状态分析问题，特别是为氢气等清洁能源输送系统提供数学模型和理论支持。

Method: 采用等温欧拉方程建立管道气流模型，通过边界控制系统框架将其转化为非线性抽象微分方程，利用提升算子进行变换，推导傅里叶系数表示，并建立抽象空间中非线性控制系统的周期解存在性条件。

Result: 建立了非线性控制系统的周期解存在条件，并将其成功应用于实际氢气输送模型，验证了周期性运行状态的存在性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效分析管道气流在周期性供需波动下的运行状态，对氢气输送系统的设计与运行优化具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: A mathematical model of gas flow in a pipeline controlled by the inlet pressure and the outlet mass flow flux is considered in the form of the isothermal Euler equations with an appropriate equation of state. Within the framework of boundary control systems, this model is transformed into a nonlinear abstract differential equation using an appropriate lifting operator. For this abstract equation, a representation in terms of Fourier coefficients is derived analytically. Conditions for the existence of periodic solutions to a broad class of nonlinear control systems with continuously differentiable controls are established in abstract spaces. This framework is applied to a realistic hydrogen transport model to evaluate periodic operating regimes under periodic fluctuations in supply and demand.

</details>


### [338] [Dynamic Cloud Service-Capacity Deployment with Costly Response Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20961)
*Ruihan Zhou,Xiaowei Zhang,Yijie Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究AI产品发布初期，在缺乏产品特定工作负载历史数据的情况下，如何联合决策当前容量部署与未来响应 readiness。提出Cold-Start Belief Actionability Policy (CBAP)策略，通过工作负载场景估计价值，避免过早部署和不必要的 readiness 支出，降低生命周期成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI产品发布时，服务容量决策面临"冷启动"问题：缺乏足够的产品特定工作负载历史数据。早期服务观测虽能更新对剩余工作负载的信念，但工作负载不像库存需求那样物理消耗容量。同时，保持快速响应能力需要昂贵的容量接入和运营安排。如何在这两者间权衡是核心动机。

Method: 构建有限时域动态规划模型，包含工作负载信念、可服务容量和吸收性响应通道状态。提出"保持-部署分解"方法：对每个续期选择求解一个部署问题，比较优化后的价值。进而提出Cold-Start Belief Actionability Policy (CBAP)，利用从预测信念中抽取的工作负载场景来估计各侧价值，并建立了决策和生命周期性能损失的边界。

Result: 数值实验和基于BurstGPT的评估表明：CBAP相比基准策略降低了生命周期成本，通过避免过早部署和不必要的 readiness 支出。结果区分了预测信息性(informativeness)与运营可操作性(actionability)，并证明了当前部署与未来响应 readiness 是两个独立的决策维度。

Conclusion: 零部署可能代表"待机"(Standby)而非"退出"(Exit)；正向部署不一定意味着需要保留响应通道。这为AI产品服务容量管理提供了新的理论框架和实用策略。

Abstract: AI product launches require service-capacity decisions before sufficient product-specific workload histories are available. Early service observations update beliefs about remaining workload, but workload does not physically deplete capacity as inventory demand does. Preserving rapid-response capability may also require costly capacity-access and operational arrangements. We study how a firm should jointly decide current capacity deployment and future response readiness. We formulate a finite-horizon dynamic program with workload beliefs, serviceable capacity, and an absorbing response-channel state. A preservation-deployment decomposition solves one deployment problem for each continuation choice and compares the resulting optimized values. This structure identifies when response readiness should be preserved and how much capacity should be deployed. We operationalize the framework through the Cold-Start Belief Actionability Policy (CBAP), which estimates side-specific values using workload scenarios drawn from predictive beliefs. We also establish bounds on its decision and lifecycle performance losses. Numerical experiments and a BurstGPT-based evaluation show that CBAP reduces lifecycle cost relative to benchmark policies by avoiding premature deployment and unnecessary readiness spending. The results distinguish forecast informativeness from operational actionability and show that current deployment and future response readiness are separate decision margins. Zero deployment may represent Standby rather than Exit, while positive deployment need not imply preserving the response channel.

</details>


### [339] [Control Co-design of systems with parabolic PDE dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20975)
*Antika Yadav,Prasad Vilas Chanekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对抛物线型偏微分方程系统的控制协同设计问题，提出了基于空间离散化和梯度优化的可计算近似求解方法，并通过实例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对PDE系统的控制与设计多分开进行，缺乏协同优化的系统化方法，计算复杂度高。

Method: 先推导PDE的充分稳定性条件，再通过空间离散化将问题转化为可计算的近似CCD问题，最后用梯度法求解。

Result: 通过实例验证了所提近似CCD方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为抛物线PDE系统的控制协同设计提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the control co-design (CCD) synthesis problem for a class of systems with parabolic partial differential equation (PDE) dynamics. We first derive a sufficient stability condition for the PDE. By spatially discretizing the PDE and using the sufficient stability condition, we propose a computationally tractable approximate CCD problem. We solve the approximate CCD problem using a gradient-based method. Finally, we justify our proposed approach through an example.

</details>


### [340] [Stabilization of 1D Linear Hyperbolic Balance Laws by Integral Difference Control and Application to Networks Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21060)
*Adam Braun,Jean Auriol,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一阶线性双曲型平衡律，提出了一种统一的指数镇定方法，涵盖欠驱动边界控制和域内控制，并通过切割与折叠变换推广到包含环路的网络系统，基于可逆反步变换和积分差分方程设计动态反馈律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅适用于特定驱动配置，缺乏统一框架处理多样化的欠驱动边界与域内控制组合，且对于包含环路的网络系统缺乏有效镇定方案。

Method: 1. 基于可逆反步变换实现系统部分解耦；2. 将镇定问题重构为积分差分方程（IDE）层面；3. 通过稳定的降秩过程将问题简化为单输入设计；4. 利用Corona问题求解插值方程构建动态反馈增益。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提方法在现有技术无法处理的环路网络上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架显著扩展了现有结果，为更广泛的双曲平衡律系统提供了系统化的镇定设计方法。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified method for the exponential stabilization of first-order linear hyperbolic balance laws under general actuation, including both underactuated boundary and in-domain control. The proposed framework brings together a wide range of underactuated configurations within a single formulation and substantially extends existing results restricted to particular actuation settings. Using cutting and folding transformations, the proposed approach is further applied to networks of hyperbolic balance laws, including configurations with cycles. The control design is developed under a stabilizability condition and a robustness assumption. It is based on an invertible backstepping transformation, which partially decouples the system, followed by a reformulation of the stabilization problem at the level of an Integral Difference Equation (IDE). The gains of the resulting dynamic feedback law are constructed at the IDE level by combining a stable rank-reduction procedure, which reduces the problem to a single-input design, with the solution of an interpolation equation arising from a Corona problem. Numerical simulations are presented for a relevant cycle network that cannot be addressed by existing methods in the literature.

</details>


### [341] [Exact ensemble controllability for neural differential equations via neural interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21112)
*Martin Gugat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经动力学系统的精确系综可控性，提出了一种基于神经插值问题的构造性解决方案，并证明当右端项为两层神经网络时，插值问题可简化为线性方程组求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度神经网络中如何用单一控制参数同时将多个不同初始状态引导至对应目标状态，这在机器学习中实现多任务学习至关重要。

Method: 将神经动力学系统的精确系综可控性问题转化为神经插值问题，通过构造性方法求解；特别地，当右侧神经网络深度为2时，将插值问题简化为线性方程组。

Result: 提出了精确系综可控性的构造性解法，并在两层神经网络条件下成功将问题转化为线性方程组求解，降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经动力学系统的可控性提供了理论基础，并为多任务学习中的参数共享问题提供了可行的数学工具。

Abstract: We study a system that is governed by neural dynamics. Neural dynamics are a model for deep neural networks with a large number of layers. For a differential equation where the right-hand side is given by a neural network, we analyze the exact ensemble controllability of the system. This property plays an essential role in Machine Learning. It concerns the ability to learn to perform different tasks simultaneously with a single neural dynamics: Exact ensemble controllability requires that $N$ different initial states are steered to corresponding $N$ target states with a single set of control parameters.
  We present a constructive solution to the problem. The construction is based on the solution of a neural interpolation problem.
  We show that if the right-hand side of the differential equation is given by a neural network of depth two, the interpolation problem can be reduced to the solution of a system of linear equations.

</details>


### [342] [A Gaussian smoothing-based zeroth-order method for Goldstein second-order stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21258)
*Ming Lei,Ting Kei Pong,Man-Chung Yue,Liaoyuan Zeng,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Goldstein二阶δ-次微分这一新概念，并定义了相应的(ε1,ε2,δ)-二阶稳定点。针对Lipschitz可微函数，设计了基于立方正则化、高斯平滑和同伦策略的零阶算法，在目标函数满足温和强制型假设下推导了迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶优化方法在处理非光滑或仅能获取零阶信息（函数值）的问题时存在局限，需要一种新的广义Hessian概念和相应的算法来找到近似二阶稳定点。

Method: 提出了Goldstein二阶δ-次微分；定义了(ε1,ε2,δ)-二阶稳定点；设计了结合立方正则化、高斯平滑与同伦策略的零阶算法。

Result: 在目标函数满足温和强制型假设下，推导了所提零阶算法找到近似二阶稳定点的迭代复杂度。

Conclusion: 新提出的Goldstein二阶δ-次微分为处理非光滑优化问题提供了新的理论工具，所设计的零阶算法在理论上具有可证明的收敛保证。

Abstract: We introduce a new generalized Hessian, called the Goldstein second-order $δ$-subdifferential, and an associated notion of $(ε_1,ε_2,δ)$-second-order stationary point for continuously differentiable functions with locally Lipschitz gradients. We propose a zeroth-order algorithm based on cubic regularization and Gaussian smoothing with homotopy to find such approximate second-order stationary points for Lipschitz differentiable functions, and derive the iteration complexity under a mild coercivity-type assumption on the objective function.

</details>


### [343] [Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov inclusions of the mean field type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21297)
*Yurii Averboukh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Wasserstein空间中由带约束的Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov方程驱动的动力系统，在系数映射满足凸值、上半连续及增长条件时，证明了系统解的存在性、解集的紧性，并研究了最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Wasserstein空间框架下研究概率测度演化动力系统，并考虑受约束的Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov方程，以处理更一般的非光滑系数情形。

Method: 假设系数多值映射是凸值的、上半连续的且满足增长条件，利用函数分析工具证明解的存在性和解集的紧性，并进一步构建最优控制框架。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下系统解的存在性，并建立了解集的紧性；此外，解决了相关最优控制问题的可解性。

Conclusion: 该研究为Wasserstein空间中带约束的测度演化动力学提供了理论基础，并为最优控制问题提供了可行性保证。

Abstract: The paper studies a dynamical system in the Wasserstein space, where the evolution is governed by a Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov equation with coefficients chosen from a prescribed set that depends at each point on that point and on the current measure. Under the assumption that the multivalued mapping determining the constraints is convex-valued, upper semicontinuous and satisfies certain growth conditions, we prove an existence theorem and establish the compactness of the solution set. Moreover, we study an optimal control problem for such dynamical systems.

</details>


### [344] [Decomposing a Multi-Scale Optimization Framework for Grid-Integrated Electrolysis using Aggregate-Informed Benders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21440)
*Kiernan X. Jennings,Victor M. Zavala,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对电解制氢系统参与需求响应时面临的计算规模难题，提出了一种聚合信息Benders分解算法，能够在长达40年的模拟中高效求解，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解设备的动态需求响应操作对系统寿命和耐久性构成挑战，且已有的多尺度优化框架在扩展到高频电力市场或长时间跨度时面临计算可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种聚合信息Benders分解方法，通过整合子问题的聚合信息加速收敛，以可扩展的方式求解大规模电网耦合电解系统的优化问题。

Result: 与商业求解器和传统Benders分解相比，该方法在40年时间跨度的实例中将最优性差距降低约81%，而传统方法在~85%处停滞。

Conclusion: 聚合信息Benders分解方法有效解决了大规模、长时间跨度的电解系统需求响应优化问题；同时发现参与实时市场在高价格波动下具有经济优势。

Abstract: Demand response (DR) operation of electrolysis devices is gaining traction to capitalize on volatile electricity markets, but their dynamic operation poses challenges to the durability and lifespan of these systems. Our recently developed multi-scale optimization framework for grid-integrated electrolysis systems studied the impacts of DR and effects on device durability. A major hurdle in that work is tractably scaling the model to include participation in high-frequency electricity markets and/or longer time horizons. To address this, in this work, we developed an aggregate-informed Benders decomposition method to tractably solve large instances of this problem structure. To illustrate the efficacy, we solve a case study with market participation in the day-ahead market (DAM) and real-time markets (RTM) for up to 40 years to effectively capture the effects of device lifespan change. We compare our decomposition algorithm to both commercial solvers and traditional Benders decomposition. We find that incorporating aggregate subproblem information accelerates convergence, reducing the final optimality gap by up to ~81% relative to traditional Benders on the 40-year horizon instances that otherwise stall near 85%. We additionally apply the algorithm to find that RTM participation offers economic advantages under the higher price volatility.

</details>


### [345] [Climate-resilient electric vehicle charging infrastructure for sustainable cities: An interpretable causal-ensemble framework for preventive maintenance and low-carbon mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21444)
*Cande Lian,Wentao Zeng,Jiabin Wu,Yiming Bie,Wei Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对极端天气（高温、暴雨、高湿）对电动汽车充电基础设施故障风险的影响，提出了一种特征调控的动态堆叠集成模型（FGDSE），该模型将异构信号分为四类特征族，结合领域专家与深度学习专家，通过视界门控机制实现1-30天的故障风险预测。在13个站点25个月的数据上，FGDSE在10天以上预测时超越12个基线模型，30天时保持约85%的宏观召回率，AUC仅下降3.2点。SHAP和X-learner分析表明极端高温是唯一因果效应随时间增强的气候因素，约30%的站点被识别为热敏感，为气候适应性维护提供了量化阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车充电基础设施在极端气候下故障风险上升，需要从被动维修转向主动预防维护，但多源异构信号的时间尺度差异和多周预测周期给精准预测带来挑战。

Method: 提出FGDSE模型：将异构信号分为四个特征族分别匹配领域专家，增加两个深度时序专家处理短期脉冲和长期退化，通过视界门控机制学习自适应权重，实现1-30天逐日故障风险预测，并结合SHAP归因和X-learner进行因果决策支持。

Result: 在13个站点25个月数据上，FGDSE在10天以上预测区间超越12个基线模型；30天时宏观召回率约85%，AUC仅下降3.2点；分析揭示故障主导因素从历史故障向气候压力转移，极端高温是唯一因果效应随时间增强的因素，约30%站点被标记为热敏感。

Conclusion: FGDSE模型能有效支持气候适应性充电资产维护，为增强城市交通韧性、保障低碳出行提供量化决策依据。

Abstract: Reliable electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure is a cornerstone of sustainable, low-carbon cities, yet urban climate stress such as extreme heat, heavy precipitation, and humidity increasingly raises equipment fault risk and undermines the resilience of urban energy and mobility services. Shifting operation from reactive repair to preventive maintenance depends on accurate, forward-looking fault-risk prediction, a task complicated by the heterogeneous time scales of physical, behavioral, contextual, and historical signals and by forecasting over a multi-week horizon. We develop FGDSE, a feature-governed dynamic stacking ensemble that forms an interpretable decision-support system for climate-resilient charging-asset management. It partitions heterogeneous signals into four feature families, assigns each to a domain expert whose inductive bias matches the data, and adds two deep temporal experts for short-term pulses and long-term degradation; a horizon-wise gating mechanism then learns adaptive weights to forecast daily fault risk over 1 to 30 days. SHAP attribution and an X-learner extend the probabilistic output into causal decision support with post-level treatment effects. On 25 months of data from 13 stations, FGDSE surpasses twelve baselines beyond the ten-day horizon, sustains about 85% macro-recall at 30 days with an AUC decay of only 3.2 points, and reveals a shift of dominance from fault history toward climate stress. It identifies extreme heat as the sole exposure whose causal effect amplifies over time, flagging roughly 30% of posts as heat-sensitive and yielding quantitative thresholds for climate-adaptive maintenance that strengthens urban mobility resilience and sustains low-carbon travel.

</details>


### [346] [Asymptotic Analysis of Empirical Dynamic Programming in Infinite-Horizon Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21520)
*Xin Chen,Elif Sena Isik,Johannes Milz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了无限时域折扣随机最优控制问题中基于样本的近似方法的统计极限定理，包括函数中心极限定理和非唯一最优策略下的非高斯极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于样本的随机最优控制近似方法提供严格的统计理论基础，理解样本近似带来的不确定性和渐近行为。

Method: 推导函数中心极限定理（唯一最优策略情形，极限为高斯过程）；推导非唯一最优策略情形的极限定理（可能非高斯）；与基于策略优化的方法进行方差比较；通过库存控制和可再生资源开采问题验证。

Result: 在唯一最优策略条件下，样本价值函数收敛到均值零的高斯过程，其协方差由类动态规划的线性不动点方程刻画；样本价值函数与策略优化的极限方差不同；非唯一最优策略下极限分布可能非高斯。

Conclusion: 为样本近似随机最优控制问题提供了完整的渐近理论，揭示了不同近似方法的方差差异，拓展到非唯一策略情形。

Abstract: We derive statistical limit theorems for sample-based approximations of infinite-horizon discounted stochastic optimal control problems in discrete time. Our first result is a functional central limit theorem for the sample-based value function under a uniqueness-type condition on population optimal policies. The limiting law is a mean-zero Gaussian process characterized by a linear fixed point equation that resembles a dynamic programming principle. We compare these asymptotics with those obtained from sample-based policy optimization and illustrate that their limiting variances can be different. We also derive a limit theorem for models with nonunique optimal policies, where the limiting law may be non-Gaussian. Applications to inventory control and renewable harvesting illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [347] [Barzilai-Borwein Fails Superlinear Convergence on an Open Set of Quadratics for Every Dimension $n\geq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21579)
*Dawei Li,Xiaotian Jiang,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明Barzilai-Borwein (BB1)方法在几乎每个严格凸二次问题和初始点上无法实现超线性收敛，通过构造一个开集（正Lebesgue测度）的反例族，其中梯度范数和误差能量范数以几何级数速率被双向控制，从而排除了超线性收敛的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BB方法在实际连续优化中表现优异，但其收敛动力学长期未被充分理解。一个核心悬而未决的问题是：BB方法是否对几乎所有严格凸二次问题和初始点都能实现超线性收敛？本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 对于n≥4的有限维，构造一个非空开集（正Lebesgue测度）的严格凸二次问题与初始点族。通过计算机辅助证明，在四维投影化BB动力学中证明存在一个非共振、吸引的七周期轨，从而建立对BB1方法收敛速率的精确几何级数上下界。

Result: 对于所构造的问题与初始点族，梯度谱分量被几何级数双向控制（ρ_min=10^{-6}, ρ_max=0.61）。梯度范数和误差能量范数满足同速率几何估计，目标间隙满足平方速率估计。所有三个量都被几何级数从下方界定，从而排除了根超线性收敛的可能性。

Conclusion: BB1方法在几乎每个严格凸二次问题和初始点上无法实现超线性收敛，给出了一个否定回答。这一结果为理解BB方法的收敛动力学提供了重要限制。

Abstract: Barzilai--Borwein (BB) method has shown strong practical performance in continuous optimization, yet its convergence dynamics remains poorly understood. In particular, a central unresolved question is whether BB converges superlinearly for almost every strictly convex quadratic problem and initialization. We provide a negative answer to this question. Specifically, for every finite dimension $n\geq4$, we construct a nonempty open, hence positive-Lebesgue-measure, family of strictly convex quadratic problems and initial points for which the long Barzilai--Borwein method (BB1) converges but cannot converge root-superlinearly. More precisely, with the explicit constants $ρ_{\min}=10^{-6},ρ_{\max}=0.61$, every spectral component of the gradient is bounded above and below by the corresponding geometric sequence. Consequently, the gradient norm and the energy norm of the error satisfy two-sided geometric estimates with the same rates, while the objective gap satisfies the corresponding estimates with squared rates. In particular, all three quantities are bounded below by geometric sequences, ruling out superlinear convergence. The construction is highly nontrivial, based on a computer-assisted proof of a nonresonant, attracting seven-cycle of the projectivized BB dynamics in dimension four.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [348] [Language Models Embody and Amplify Human Cognitive Distortions: What Is to Be Done?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20695)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Steven A. Lehr,Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM存在隐蔽且严重的社会认知偏见：偏见是AI对齐功能的副产品，不仅反映而且放大人类偏见，随着模型代际更迭而加剧，甚至能将偏见传递给人类。作者提出诊断、监管和操作层面的应对措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类判断本质上容易出错，AI被寄予消除偏见、确保公平的期望，但研究表明LLM存在严重的社会认知偏见，这需要引起学界和社会的高度警惕。

Method: 通过综述现有研究证据，系统分析LLM偏见的四个特征（隐蔽性、放大性、代际加剧性、可传递性），并提出三类应对措施。

Result: 发现偏见是AI对齐目标的意外产物，模型越对齐偏见越隐蔽；AI对偏见不是简单镜像而是放大；新一代模型偏见更严重；人类与有偏见的AI交互后自身偏见也会增加。

Conclusion: 需要从诊断、监管和操作三个层面采取综合对策，以应对AI偏见对决策的潜在深远影响。

Abstract: Human judgment is fundamentally prone to error. A promise of AI is that it will rid decisions of bias and ensure a fairer and safer world for all. Yet research unequivocally demonstrates that LLMs exhibit consequential sociocognitive biases. We alert readers that bias in AI (a) is covert and ironically a feature of alignment goals, (b) is not merely a mirror, but an amplifier of human bias, (c) intensifies across model generations, and (d) even transmits bias to humans. Given the potentially seismic and ubiquitous influence of AI on decision making, we propose countermeasures that are diagnostic, regulatory and operational.

</details>


### [349] [Open Veins of Algorithmic Auditing: Why AI Assessment Lags Behind Its Deployment in the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21317)
*Gemma Galdon Clavell,Alexandra Magaard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于十年间在拉丁美洲、撒哈拉以南非洲和亚太地区的AI审计实践，揭示了全球南方AI治理的严重缺失：过去十年仅有不到20项已发表的第三方审计，而数百个公共算法已部署。研究发现四个模式（代理目标、性能崩塌、数据偏倚、结构性偏见），并提出评估缺口本质是资金问题而非能力问题，建议发展机构和慈善资助方通过资助条件强制要求独立评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方AI部署速度与北方相当甚至更快，但AI治理严重滞后，且该地区AI评估实践极为稀少。作者希望通过系统梳理十年审计实践，记录全球南方AI评估的模式特征，解释为何评估如此匮乏，并提出可行的解决路径。

Method: 基于十年AI审计实践（包括公开审计如巴西Robot Laura、两项已完成未发布的国家级审计、13项负责任AI评估、区域景观分析），统计已发表的第三方审计数量，归纳跨领域模式，并从中提炼评估、监管和资金方面的五条经验教训。

Result: 过去十年全球南方仅有不到20项已发表的第三方审计，发现四个模式：代理目标以预测性替代有效性、性能声称在患病率分析下坍塌、评分模型从未见过训练数据中的人群、移除敏感属性后结构性偏见依然存在。指出评估缺口本质是资金问题而非能力问题。

Conclusion: 全球南方AI评估缺口的根源是资金问题：能力跟随有资金的需需求，目前没有行为体被要求或有资金来问责已部署系统。最合适的补救者是少数发展机构和慈善资助方，他们可通过资助条件要求独立评估。最后向资助方、政府和评估设计者提出建议。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is being deployed across the Global South at a pace matching or exceeding the Global North, yet AI governance has not kept pace, and the gap is far wider in the South. Drawing on a decade of AI audit practice across Latin America, Sub-Saharan Africa, and Asia Pacific (the only fully published second-party audit of a deployed system in the region, Robot Laura in Brazil; two completed but unreleased national audits, of a child-welfare risk model and a public-employment matching algorithm; thirteen Responsible AI Assessments; and a regional landscape analysis), this paper documents patterns in the sparse field of Global South AI evaluation and explains why it so rarely occurs. We count fewer than twenty published second- and third-party audits of deployed systems across the region over the past decade, against hundreds of documented public-sector algorithms and multibillion-dollar national AI investments. We find four cross-cutting patterns: proxy targets that substitute predictability for validity, performance claims that collapse under prevalence analysis, populations scored by models that never saw them in training, and structural bias that persists even after protected attributes are removed. We then draw five lessons for evaluation, regulation, and funding that emerge outside the regulatory, linguistic, and data conditions of the North. We argue the gap is not, at root, a capacity problem but a funding problem: capacity follows funded demand, and no actor is currently required, or funded, to hold deployed systems to account. The actor best placed to close it is the small number of development and philanthropic funders behind most consequential AI in the region, whose funding conditions can require independent evaluation where no regulator yet does. We close with recommendations for funders, governments, and the design of evaluation itself.

</details>


### [350] [White Box Evidence Packages for Policy Audit Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21462)
*Seunghyun Yoo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在AI治理中，如何判断LLM生成的审计报告是否真正有证据支持。通过控制证据接口，发现内部模型证据会改变报告引用和推理方式，但更多内部引用并不必然提升报告有效性；混合接口平均最有用，而打乱控制暴露了关键治理风险：报告可能听起来合理但实际上引用了无关的内部证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI治理从依赖基准分数转向可审计的监督，核心问题在于审查者如何判断LLM生成的审计报告是否真正有证据支持。现有研究缺乏对证据接口如何影响审计报告质量的系统性理解。

Method: 提出受控评估框架，固定段落、评分标准和审计模型，仅改变提供给审计者的证据接口。在60个AGORA政策案例中，在10种证据条件下生成600份结构化报告，由5名人类评审者评估正确性、段落关联性、诊断有用性和证据误用情况。

Result: 内部证据改变了报告的引用和推理方式，但更多内部引用不提升有效性；白盒诊断揭示了因果定位狭窄、报告更易复用可读标签和标记方向；混合接口平均最有用；打乱控制暴露了治理风险：报告可听起来合理却引用无关内部证据。

Conclusion: 应将内部模型访问重新定位为审计工作流中的证据设计问题，而非透明度的保证。

Abstract: As AI governance moves from benchmark scores toward auditable oversight, a central question is how reviewers can tell whether an LLM-generated audit report is actually supported by evidence. This paper studies that question in passage-anchored policy audits, where a report must interpret a given policy passage and cite evidence for its claims. We introduce a controlled evaluation framework that holds the passage, rubric, and auditor model fixed while changing only the evidence interface supplied to the auditor. Across 60 AGORA policy cases, we generate 600 structured reports under ten evidence conditions, including passage-based evidence, internal model evidence, a hybrid package, and a shuffled control that preserves evidence format while breaking case relevance. Five human reviewers evaluate the primary interfaces for correctness, passage grounding, diagnostic usefulness, and evidence misuse. The results show that internal evidence changes how reports cite and reason about evidence, but more internal citations do not by themselves make a report more valid. A white-box diagnostic explains the failure mode: causal localization is narrow, while reports readily reuse broader readable labels and token directions. The hybrid interface is the most useful on average, while the shuffled control exposes a key governance risk: reports can sound substantively plausible while citing irrelevant internal evidence. This study reframes internal model access as an evidence design problem for audit workflows, rather than as a guarantee of transparency.

</details>


### [351] [Generative AI Availability, Grades, and Student Satisfaction at a Large University](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21534)
*James M. Zumel Dumlao,Meng Wang,Zhonghan Xie,Junyao Hu,Ivan Bar,George Chaney,Henry Gold,Misha Teplitskiy*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于2015-2025年某大型美国大学的数据（156,135名学生，87,936门课程），研究发现生成式AI的普及并未显著导致AI易感课程的成绩上升，也未显著降低学生的理解度和兴趣，从而削弱了对“GenAI替代假说”的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证“GenAI替代假说”——学生是否因使用生成式AI完成作业而获得更高分数但并未真正学习，从而引发对高等教育评估诚信与学习质量的担忧。

Method: 利用LLM管道从教学大纲中提取评估类型以衡量课程的GenAI易感性，采用双重差分法比较ChatGPT发布前后不同课程的结果差异，同时建模COVID-19效应的持续性或短暂性。

Result: GenAI可用性对总体成绩及以往低分学生成绩均无显著差异效应；对自报理解度的影响也不显著；对兴趣的影响仅在假设疫情效应为短暂性时显著。

Conclusion: 研究结果缓解了关于GenAI抬高成绩并降低学生满意度的担忧，表明该替代假说缺乏实证支持。

Abstract: The spread of generative AI (GenAI) in higher education has raised concerns that students offload cognitive effort to AI, earning high grades without learning. If this "GenAI substitution hypothesis" is true, grades should rise disproportionately in GenAI-susceptible courses--those relying more on assessments like take-home problem sets and essays rather than in-class exams. Substitution could also affect student satisfaction, measured here as self-reported understanding and interest in the subject, which prior research links to assessments. We test the substitution hypothesis using syllabus and administrative data from a large U.S. university (2015-2025; 156,135 students; 87,936 course offerings). We measure courses' GenAI susceptibility using a human-validated LLM pipeline to extract assessment types from syllabi, and use a differences-in-differences design comparing outcomes across courses before and after ChatGPT's release, while modeling COVID-19 pandemic effects as either persistent or transient. We find no significant differential effect of GenAI availability on grades overall or among previously lower-performing students. Effects on self-reported understanding are likewise insignificant; effects on interest are significant only assuming transient pandemic effects. Our findings temper concerns that GenAI inflates grades and reduces students' satisfaction.

</details>


### [352] [Seeking Help in the Digital Age: A Cross-Platform Analysis of Online Support Systems for Technology-Facilitated Abuse Victims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21549)
*Nowshin Tabassum,Solomon G. Dandekar,Morgan PettyJohn,Tim Ryan,Minjaal Raval,Rachel Voth Schrag,Mohit Singhal,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对网络搜索、同伴支持论坛和对话式AI系统三种渠道上针对技术辅助虐待受害者的在线支持进行了大规模评估，发现各平台支持质量参差不齐，均未能提供安全、创伤知情的一致支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术辅助虐待已成为普遍的人际伤害形式，受害者在寻求在线指导时，获得的回应会直接影响其风险评估、虐待认知和保护行为选择，因此有必要系统评估现有数字支持渠道的质量。

Method: 基于十年间r/Stalking论坛中的受害者叙述，使用定性编码和有监督分类器构建涵盖11类技术滥用的TFA查询数据集；在三种渠道上模拟这些查询，并采用统一框架从技术、社会和安全维度评估响应质量。

Result: Google搜索和通用LLM比Reddit论坛提供更相关和可操作性强的指导；搜索中超过65%的查询遇到恶意链接；Reddit中超过20%的讨论包含毒性回应；AI系统常未能提供风险感知指导或具体支持资源；领域特定的幸存者支持聊天机器人表现甚至不如通用LLM。

Conclusion: 现有数字支持渠道对TFA受害者存在显著弱点，亟需以安全为中心的设计、评估和部署未来支持技术。

Abstract: Technology-facilitated abuse (TFA), the use of digital technologies to stalk, harass, monitor or threaten others, has become a pervasive form of interpersonal harm. As victims turn to online sources for guidance, responses can shape how they assess risks, interpret abuse, and choose protective actions. We present a large-scale evaluation of online support for TFA victims across three channels: web search, peer-support forums, and conversational AI systems. Drawing on a decade of victim narratives from r/Stalking, we use qualitative coding and supervised classifiers to construct a dataset of TFA queries spanning 11 categories of technology misuse. We simulate these queries across the three channels and evaluate responses using a unified framework spanning technical, social, and safety dimensions. The framework assesses relevance, accuracy, actionability, persuasiveness, and understandability, alongside platform risks and support characteristics, including social-engineering risk, toxicity, empathy, bias, risky guidance, and support information. We build and validate automated classifiers to scale the evaluation. Our findings reveal differences in support quality across platforms. Google Search and general-purpose LLMs provide more relevant and actionable guidance than Reddit discussions, yet none consistently provide safe, trauma-informed support. More than 65% of victim queries encounter potentially malicious links in search results, over 20% of Reddit discussions contain toxic responses, and conversational AI systems frequently fail to provide risk-aware guidance or concrete support resources. Surprisingly, domain-specific survivor-support chatbots underperform general-purpose LLMs across most dimensions. These findings expose weaknesses in digital support for TFA victims and highlight the need for safety-centered design, evaluation, and deployment of future support technologies.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [353] [Quantifying Sub-Optimality in Routing for Automated Market Makers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20762)
*Weiye Xi,Ciamac C. Moallemi*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文对以太坊上298万笔WETH-USDC互换交易进行了大规模实证审计，发现DEX路由平均每笔交易损失2.02个基点（总计约2400万美元），并提出了三种可复现的最优基准来归因损失，揭示了信息时效性和攻击行为是路由次优性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DEX路由优化研究缺乏大规模实证审计，且缺乏可复现的基准来量化路由损失并归因其来源（如拆分质量、池子覆盖范围、Gas成本等）。

Method: 使用298万笔真实WETH-USDC互换数据，提出三种最优基准（SCO、FVO、G-FVO），并设计基于二分搜索的跨池最优路由算法以支持大规模计算。

Result: 1) 信息时效性至关重要：使用过时状态（一区块延迟）会显著增加损失；2) 低效性呈现异质性和重尾分布：小交易百分比损失更高，少数极端异常值主导总美元损失；3) 三明治攻击是路由次优性的重要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 提出了可复现的基准协议和算法，为评估和改进信息及时、Gas感知的路由提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: We provide a large-scale empirical audit of DEX routing using 2.98 million WETH-USDC swaps on Ethereum. Comparing realized routes with optimized benchmarks, we measure an average shortfall of 2.02 bps per trade or \$24 million. To attribute losses, we introduce three reproducible optimal benchmarks: a Support-Constrained Optimum (SCO) that evaluates split quality conditional on the pools actually used; a Full-Venue Optimum (FVO) that considers all available pools to quantify the value of broader pool access; and a Gas-Aware FVO (G-FVO) that augments FVO with gas costs to capture the trade-off between additional pool usage and gas expenditure. Computing these benchmarks at scale is enabled by a bisection-based algorithm for optimal routing across multiple pools for the same token pair. Two regularities emerge. First, information timeliness is crucial: moving from execution-time state to one-block lagged state optimization significantly raises mean shortfall and additional delays further degrade performance, albeit with diminishing increments; evaluated on the same stale snapshots, realized routes lie closer to optimal, indicating timing-mismatch as a key component. Second, inefficiency is heterogeneous and heavy-tailed: small trades suffer higher percentage losses, while a few extreme outliers dominate the aggregate dollar shortfalls. Finally, we demonstrate that sandwiching attacks drive a significant fraction of routing sub-optimality. Our benchmark protocol and algorithm offer a rigorous, reproducible basis for evaluating and improving information-timely, gas-aware routing.

</details>
